# v5 Decisions log

Locked decisions live in `MASTER-PLAN.md` §2 and §10. This file records every call an agent had to make on its own.
Format: `- **D-##** (M#, role) DEFAULTED — <decision>. Why: <reason>. Overrule: <what changes if Kevin disagrees>.`

- **D-01** (M0, orchestrator) DEFAULTED — The v4 harness (text parity against Figma, v4 click-throughs, Figma-ref diffs) is archived to `scripts/v4/` and no longer runs. v5's `npm run check` = v5 click-throughs (Test-flows entries + cross-persona sync) + visual regression against code-rendered baselines in `ref/v5/` + style hygiene + a no-"%"-in-money lint. Why: v5 is code-first, there is no Figma token, and v4 assertions describe retired behaviour. Overrule: re-port parity once v5 frames exist in Figma.
- **D-02** (M0, orchestrator) DEFAULTED — The v4 screens are unregistered in M0, so between M0 and M1 the app boots to a v5 index/placeholder screen. v4 stays intact on `main`. Why: v5 replaces the state model the v4 screens depend on. Overrule: none needed — v4 is reachable from `main`.
- **D-03** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Money is stored and computed in integer cents (`js/money.js`); breakdown, receipt and payout rows print exact cents ("$16.00"), price lists / list-row estimates / the rush ladder print whole dollars when whole ("$25"); negatives use U+2212 ("Taily fee −$12.50"). Why: 10 % of a $45 service is $4.50, so cents are unavoidable; one rule per surface keeps columns aligned. Overrule: change `fmt` / `fmtExact` in `js/money.js`.
- **D-04** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The courier charge never appears on the tailor's side (no row in the tailor breakdown, not part of payout); the courier's 10 % markup is Taily revenue. Why: the customer pays it and it goes to the courier + Taily; showing it to the tailor would inflate "order total". Overrule: add a muted "Courier (paid by customer)" row to `tailorBreakdown`.
- **D-05** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — When a customer cancels within 24 h, the kept deposit is paid to the tailor in full, with no Taily fee taken. Why: the doc says the deposit "covers the non-refundable home-visit fee", which is the tailor's. Overrule: route the kept deposit (or part of it) to Taily in `cancelTerms`.
- **D-06** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — On a customer no-show the deposit is kept and the tailor is paid the larger of the deposit and the $25 no-show compensation (`noShowPayout = max(deposit, $25)`). Why: keeps v4's $25 guarantee without paying twice when the deposit already exceeds it. Overrule: pay deposit + $25, or a flat $25.
- **D-07** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — "Try again ×2" = up to three attempts in total; the tailor is hidden from the list for that job only when the request expires with both retries used (the second Try again also expired). Why: reconciles §2 "Try again ×2 then other tailors" with §10 "after the 2nd expiry"; C7 lists Try again 1/2 and 2/2 as distinct states. Overrule: hide after the second expiry (one fewer attempt) by setting `MAX_RETRIES: 1` and relabelling.
- **D-08** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — A tailor who declined a job stays in "Tailors near you" for that same job as a dimmed row with the note "Declined this job" and cannot be booked for it; any other job can book them normally. Why: the doc allows re-requesting "for a different job"; with a one-tailor seed, hiding the row would make the declined state indistinguishable from "no tailors". Overrule: hide declined tailors for that job.
- **D-09** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The prototype runs on a frozen demo clock (Tue, Oct 13, 2026 · 10:00 AM, `state.clock`); countdowns, the 24 h window and expiries move only when a Test-flows step or a transition moves the clock — nothing ticks in real time. Why: deterministic screenshots and click-throughs; every timer state stays reachable from the menu. Overrule: add a 1-minute interval that calls `advanceClock(1)` outside `?test=1`.
- **D-10** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Visual baselines are one per Test-flows key (`ref/v5/<key>.png`), not one per screen id. "Sibling" now means "gets its own Figma frame at sync"; in code every state is the same screen file reached by its flow key (no extra registered routes). Why: covers every state (overlays included) with one mechanism and keeps one file per layout. Overrule: register sibling ids and baseline by id.
- **D-11** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — A job holds at most 10 garments (`MAX_ITEMS`). Why: C1 lists a "max items" state without a number; 10 fits one home visit and the tile grid. Overrule: change `MAX_ITEMS` in `js/data.js`.
- **D-12** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Reschedule is available only more than 24 h before the visit; the customer picks another open slot of the same tailor and the booking stays Confirmed immediately (the tailor is notified, no re-approval). Within 24 h the customer can message or cancel. Why: open slots are the tailor's own availability, and a re-approval loop would duplicate the request flow. Overrule: send reschedules back through `requested`.
- **D-13** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The machine screen inventory lives in `js/inventory.js` (importable by the browser index screen and by Node), not `scripts/screens.json`, which is archived with the v4 harness. Why: `sys-index` and the harness both need it without a fetch. Overrule: generate a JSON copy for tools that need one.
- **D-14** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — A suggested time has no expiry; the deposit hold stays authorized until the customer accepts (captured) or picks another tailor (released). Why: the doc gives no window and the customer is the one who must act. Overrule: add `SUGGESTION_EXPIRY_H` to `POLICY`.
- **D-15** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — An expiry releases the deposit hold; Try again re-authorizes the same amount on the same card. Why: §2 says expired → hold released in full. Overrule: keep the hold through retries.
- **D-16** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The Taily fee and the deposit are rounded half-up to the cent once per order, not per line. Why: the tailor sees one fee row per job. Overrule: per-line rounding in `money.js`.
- **D-17** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Visits are 30 min at the shop and 60 min at home, start on :00 / :30 between 9 AM and 9 PM, and the earliest bookable slot is 3 h from now (`MIN_LEAD_H`) so the 2 h reply window ends before the visit. Why: needed to generate open slots; not in the doc. Overrule: edit `VISIT_MINUTES` / `POLICY.MIN_LEAD_H`.
- **D-18** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — A visit must be at least one calendar day before the need-by date; slots on or after the need-by are not offered ("nothing open before need-by" when none remain), and a need-by before tomorrow is a field error. Why: the tailor needs the garment after the fitting; the rush ladder starts at "≤1 day". Overrule: allow same-day (treat as the 1-day rush tier).
- **D-19** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — "At the shop" lists shop-enabled tailors within 5 miles of the customer's zip (mock `ZIP_INFO` distances); home visits use only the tailor's zip list. Why: the doc filters home visits by zip but says nothing for shop visits. Overrule: change `SHOP_RADIUS_MI`.
- **D-20** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Tailor onboarding Test-flows steps reset the seed tailor to a blank profile with the same id (`amara`), so what the tailor enters is what Sarah sees in the list. Why: one-tailor seed and the cross-persona demo. Overrule: onboard a second, separate tailor.
- **D-21** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — `state.persona` is `'customer' | 'tailor'` (v4 used `'user'`); `sys-*` screens keep the current persona. Why: matches the v5 docs' vocabulary. Overrule: none needed.
- **D-22** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The customer can withdraw a pending (or suggested-time) request from the request screen; the hold is released in full and the job returns to the list. Why: C6 has no exit otherwise for a 2 h wait. Overrule: remove the Withdraw action.
- **D-23** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — After a price change the deposit stays as captured; the balance is the new total minus the deposit, and if the new total is below the deposit the difference is refunded at Ready. Why: §2 fixes the deposit; a large removal could otherwise make the balance negative. Overrule: recompute the deposit.
- **D-24** (M0, spec writer) DEFAULTED — A tailor's payout is created as pending when the job is marked Completed and is paid out weekly (the following Friday); kept deposits and no-show pay follow the same schedule. Why: T8 needs pending / paid / failed states and the doc gives no timing. Overrule: pay on completion.
- **D-25** (M0, model worker) DEFAULTED — The price is fixed when the request is sent: rescheduling or accepting a suggested time keeps the booked quote, even if the new visit date would fall in a higher rush tier. Why: the customer agreed (and the deposit was held) on that total; only the tailor's post-fitting price change moves it. Overrule: recompute `rush` from the new slot in `rescheduleBooking` / `acceptSuggestion`.
- **D-26** (M0, model worker) DEFAULTED — `approvePriceChange` also works after the customer has messaged the tailor (`priceChange.state === 'messaged'`); `messageAboutPriceChange` works once. Why: §2 says disputes settle in chat, so the customer must still be able to approve afterwards. Overrule: require `pending` for approve.
- **D-27** (M0, model worker) DEFAULTED — A tailor who is not fully onboarded (any of the six flags false) is treated like an out-of-area tailor in `eligible` / `tailorsFor` (reason `zip` for home visits, `shop-off` for shop visits). Why: D-20 resets the seed tailor to blank during onboarding and the list must then be empty without a new empty-state kind. Overrule: add a `not-bookable` empty state.
- **D-28** (M0, model worker) DEFAULTED — `openSlots` / `nextOpening` return ISO start strings (`'2026-10-15T17:00'`), not objects; the end is `start + VISIT_MINUTES[visitType]`. `money.estimate(tailor, job, slotIso)` reads the rush tier from the slot it is given (null → no rush); `state.estimateNext(tailor, job)` passes the tailor's next opening. Why: keeps `money.js` free of state and matches the `slotStart` arguments of the transitions. Overrule: return `{ start, end }` objects.
- **D-29** (M0, component builder) DEFAULTED — `customerBreakdown` copy the spec left open: the estimate stage labels the total "Estimated total" (later stages "Total"); the Balance status reads "Due when ready" until Ready, "Due now" at the Ready stage, "Paid" after; the Services row carries the caption "<Tailor>’s prices" when `tailorFirst` is passed; a refund row ("Refund to your card") appears at Ready / Paid when `quote.refund > 0` (D-23). Why: C-stage screens need a distinct word for money that is due right now, and a refund must be visible where the balance would have been. Overrule: edit the `customerBreakdown` row table in `js/components.js`.
- **D-30** (M0 review fixer) DEFAULTED — `customerBreakdown` takes the deposit word from the appointment, not the stage: `depositState` (`authorized` "On hold" · `captured` "Paid" · `released` "Released" · `refunded` "Refunded" · `kept` "Kept"). A pending request is a hold that decline / expiry / withdraw can release (§2: authorized at request, captured on accept), so c-request and c-pay pass `appt.payments.deposit.state`. Without `depositState` the old stage words stay (estimate "Due today", later stages "Paid"). Why: "Paid" on a pending request contradicted "held, not charged". Overrule: edit `DEPOSIT_STATUS` in `js/components.js`.
- **D-31** (M0 review fixer) DEFAULTED — `tryAgain` re-checks the slot is still open (`slot-unavailable`) before re-authorizing, and `openSlots` builds each start from wall-clock fields (`setHours`) so a daylight-saving changeover day keeps its hours. The visual gate is 0.10 % of pixels AND at most 2000 mismatching pixels (`MAX_PX` in `scripts/v5/diff.mjs`). A missing `runFlow` or an empty registry now fails flows and visual (`--allow-empty` for a scratch run). Why: review findings R0-02, R0-03, R0-04, R0-08. Overrule: change the guard / constant.
- **D-32** (M0, Figma sync) DEFAULTED — Figma node ids for v5 live in a new `scripts/screens.json` holding only a `v5` key (`pages`, `boards`, `components`, `screens`); the v4 file stays archived at `scripts/v4/screens.json` and the screen inventory stays in `js/inventory.js` (D-13). The v5 masters are prefixed `v5 / …` in a new Components section "V5 — M0", built with variants for visual states (not Fee-Row-style variable modes) and text overrides for content; `chatBubble` nests the existing T2 / Chat Bubble, `emptyState` / `readOnlyBanner` nest CTA, review / chat photos nest Photo. Why: the sync needs a machine-readable id map without reviving the v4 harness file; variants keep each state inspectable for review. Overrule: move the map into `js/inventory.js`, or convert content states to variable modes.
- **D-33** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Sign-in is simulated in a new DOM-free `js/account.js`: any 6 digits verify except `000000` (the demo's wrong code); a code works for 10 min (`POLICY.CODE_TTL_MIN`) and Resend unlocks after 30 s (`POLICY.CODE_RESEND_S`); Sarah's number is an existing account (skips the name step), any other number is a new account (→ c-name), and sign-up renames the one seed customer record, keeping the seed address and card; Apple / Google sign in as Sarah. Why: no model worker in M1, the demo needs every error state reachable, and a fresh record would strand the demo without an address or card. Overrule: give new accounts an empty address/cards list in `saveName`.
- **D-34** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Text a user has typed but not submitted (phone, code, names, card fields) lives in `state.ui['<screen id>']`; Test-flows setups may seed it to stand in for typing, then press the real CTA via `click`. Setups never seed model data. Why: the harness clicks but cannot type, and `resetDemo()` already clears `state.ui`. Overrule: add a `type` step to the FLOWS contract.
- **D-35** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — `resetDemo()` restores `state.sim.latency` to the boot value (instant under `?test=1`, else normal), and every simulated network wait goes through `withLatency` (new `js/sim.js`: instant → synchronous, normal → 700 ms, hold → never). Why: `reset()` keeps latency, so a flow that set `'hold'` for a loading / busy state would leak into every later flow. Overrule: per-screen busy flags in `state.ui` instead of the latency knob.
- **D-36** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The booking draft that spans screens (chosen tailor, slot, payment method) lives in `state.ui.booking = { tailorId, slot, cardId }`; each reader defaults when missing (first tailor, first open slot, default card) and c-pay clears it on a sent request. Why: screens can't pass params and the model's `state.job` holds only the job. Overrule: add `tailorId` / `slot` to `state.job`.
- **D-37** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The booking step indicator has 5 steps — What needs work · Where and when · Tailors near you · Tailor · Book — with c-book and c-pay both on step 5; sign-in, c-request and c-thread show none. It is a compact, non-interactive bar ("2 of 5"), and each step's primary action sits in a sticky bottom action bar. Why: CORE-JOURNEY promises "5 screens" to a sent request; picking a time and paying are one "Book" step. Overrule: make c-book its own step (6 total).
- **D-38** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Before the request is sent, c-pay labels the deposit "Held today" (new `depositState: 'to-hold'`), the CTA reads "Hold $16.00 and send request" (amount from `estimate().deposit`), and an explainer states the hold is charged only on accept and released in full on decline / no reply within `REQUEST_EXPIRY_H`, with free cancellation until `CANCEL_WINDOW_H` before the visit. Why: §2 authorizes at request and captures on accept; "Pay" / "Due today" would read as a charge. Overrule: edit `DEPOSIT_STATUS['to-hold']` and the c-pay CTA label.
- **D-39** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — c-where shows "Rush fees may apply" when any tailor in `tailorsFor(job)` has a rush fee in their estimate, not after a fixed day count. Why: the ladder is per tailor and the visit date is not chosen yet; this never warns falsely. Overrule: warn whenever the need-by is within the largest ladder tier.
- **D-40** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — A simulated card decline sticks to the declined method (retrying it fails again); switching to another saved method or adding a card clears it (`account.useOtherMethod` / `addCard` set `sim.card = 'ok'`). Cancelling 3-D Secure holds nothing and shows an info banner. Why: "card kept" means the same card stays selected, and the demo must be able to recover. Overrule: keep the decline until the knob is reset.
- **D-41** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — c-request's "Withdraw request" action and sheet are built in M1 (its key `c-request--withdraw` is tagged M3 in the inventory, the entry is added now). Why: D-22 — a 2-hour wait with a held deposit needs an exit from day one. Overrule: hide the action until M3.
- **D-42** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Garment photos are optional, counted per garment (`photos` via `setJob`), at most 3 per garment; Take photo and Choose from library both add a placeholder photo in the prototype. Why: C1 lists "photo add" without a limit; 3 matches the review photo limit. Overrule: change the screen constant in `c-job.js`.
- **D-43** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The c-tailors sort sheet offers Recommended (default) · Lowest price · Nearest · Soonest opening; the choice shows on the Sort button and re-orders rows (no visible change with the one seed tailor). Filters are edited on c-where, reached from the filter pills. Why: §3 lists "filter/sort" and the filters are exactly C2's three answers. Overrule: add in-list filters.
- **D-44** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The tailor profile leads with "Your estimate" (this job's lines at her prices, home-visit fee, rush, estimated total, "confirmed at the fitting") above her full price list, which shows 4 services then "See all 10 prices"; her rush ladder is shown in dollars ("1 day $40 · … · 4+ days free"); the courier is described as "priced when your order is ready, before you pay". Why: the decision Sarah makes is about her job, not the catalog; progressive disclosure keeps the profile short. Overrule: show the full list first.
- **D-45** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The c-job services sheet is multi-select, shows no prices (prices are per tailor), and allows saving none; "at least one service per garment" is enforced when Next is pressed. Why: SCREENS.md says "prices not shown yet"; errors belong at submit. Overrule: require a pick before Done.
- **D-46** (M1, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The M1 "Ask a question" thread (c-thread) sends real messages through `T.sendMessage` (masking applies) and offers three suggestion chips that fill the composer; full thread rules arrive in M5. Why: a stub that cannot send would be a dead end on the profile's secondary action. Overrule: disable sending until M5.
- **D-47** (M1, integrator) DEFAULTED — `render()` in `js/app.js` hands every `wire(root)` a FRESH `#screen` node (same id and attributes, `cloneNode(false)` + `replaceWith`), so a screen's `root.addEventListener(...)` can no longer stack across renders or double-fire on a same-id `go()`. The Test-flows harness counts overlays mounted inside `#screen` (sheetOverlay / modalOverlay) and requires one only for inventory states of kind `overlay` (a `click` may also press an inline control, e.g. `c-job--no-service`). Why: three M1 builders hit the leak independently and worked around it per screen. Overrule: keep the persistent node and require each screen to bind to its own render's nodes.
- **D-48** (M1, integrator) DEFAULTED — c-tailors, when every tailor is hidden (`tailorsFor` → `none-left`), shows "You have seen every tailor near you" · "Change your ZIP or need-by date to see more." · "Change details" (→ c-where); a shop-visit job outside the shop's range reads "No shops near <zip> yet" with "Switch to At my place". c-request avoids gendered pronouns ("Amara has 2 hours to reply") because the seed has no pronoun field. Why: the M1 spec did not cover these states; M3 owns the `none-left` flow entry and may refine the copy. Overrule: edit the strings in `js/screens/c-tailors.js` / `c-request.js`.
- **D-49** (M1, review fixer) DEFAULTED — The customer total reads "Estimated total" for `estimate` AND `booked` (a requested or accepted job), and "Total" only once the price is confirmed (`customerBreakdown(..., { confirmed: true })` at the fitting or after an approved price change) or at `ready` / `paid`. This overrules D-29 for requested / confirmed. Why: §0 and §E say the price is only confirmed at the fitting, so a not-yet-accepted request must not read as a final price. Overrule: drop the `booked` branch of `est` in `customerBreakdown`.
- **D-50** (M1, review fixer) DEFAULTED — The cancellation promise is computed from the chosen slot with `cancelTerms()`, never fixed copy. c-pay: more than `CANCEL_WINDOW_H` out reads "Free cancellation until <cutoff>. After that, the <deposit> deposit is kept."; inside the window it reads (warning glyph) "This visit is less than 24 hours away. If you cancel after <tailor> accepts, the <deposit> deposit is kept." c-request repeats the late sentence. Why: the first shop slot is 3 hours after the demo clock, so "free cancellation" was a promise the model would not keep. Overrule: block slots inside the window instead.
- **D-51** (M1, review fixer) DEFAULTED — A sheet drops its header ✓ (`sheet / sheetOverlay(..., { confirm: false })`) whenever it has its own CTAs or commits on tap: withdraw, 3-D Secure, services, photo, address, sort, pay-with (and add-card). The header keeps ✕ only (aria-label "Close"); a ✓ ("Done") stays only on a sheet where it is the sole commit. Warning-coloured TEXT uses the new `--warning-ink` token (the base `--warning` stays for glyphs and strokes). Serif headings use lining figures. Why: R1-1-C-02, -03, -12. Overrule: pass `confirm: true`; edit the token.
- **D-52** (M1, review fixer) DEFAULTED — c-pay's title reads "Review and send request", not the spec's "Review and pay deposit" (M1-spec c-pay layout and expect, overruled). Why: D-38 — before the request is sent the deposit is only authorized ("Held today", "Hold $16.00 and send request", charged only if the tailor accepts); §0 money honesty asks for the right word for what happens to the money, and the title is the first and largest text on the screen. The amount stays in the breakdown and the CTA, not the heading. Overrule: edit the `headingRow` in `js/screens/c-pay.js` and the `FLOWS_c_pay` expects. Also (R1-2-C-01 / -03 / -04): Scheduler wheel columns are keyboard listboxes (Day / Hour / Minute / AM/PM; arrows, Page keys, Home / End) and the rush note is a polite live region; the need-by sheet opens with a "Need it by" header and ✕ like every other sheet; meta rows use the shared 16 px line icons (`metaRow(glyph, text, { icon: 'shop'|'home'|'bag'|'truck' })`) instead of Unicode glyphs.
- **D-53** (M1, Figma sync) DEFAULTED — M1 frames live on `User - v5` in the section "M1 — Customer booking" (16 frames, the inventory's M1 `frame` states, journey order left→right; c-thread--new last as the side path from the profile). `c-request--suggested / --declined / --expired` are M3 in `js/inventory.js` (SCREENS.md lists them without an M tag), so they wait for the M3 sync. Each frame is 390 wide and as tall as the rendered page (844 minimum; c-job 1281, c-tailor 2714, c-pay 1241, c-request 1287) so the whole scroll is reviewable, with the sticky action bar at the bottom. Frames are built from the rendered DOM (real copy and dollar amounts from the app) as absolutely positioned layers, and a v5 / v4 master instance replaces a block only when its text slots match the DOM one to one (CTA, Code Input, Tailor Row, Rating Summary, Money Row, Privacy Note); otherwise the block is drawn with bound tokens. Every text fill, size and family and every rectangle / frame fill and stroke is bound to Taily variables; D-51's `--warning-ink` was added to Taily / Colors as `semantic/warning-ink` (#7A5A1E) so the request pill binds. Raw values left: the selected garment tile's drop shadow (black at 0.25 alpha), SVG clip-mask fills, radii 1 / 1.5 / 22 / 26.5 with no token. Ids in `scripts/screens.json` → `v5.screens`. Why: no auto-layout reconstruction from CSS in one pass, and parity with the shipped screen beats an approximate rebuild. Overrule: rebuild the screens as auto-layout stacks of masters in M6.
- **D-54** (M1, design-director polish) DEFAULTED — Director calls after the M1 review (`.shots/M1-p3.json`): (1) `tailorRow` makes the estimate the focal element ("Est. $160", 24 / 600 tabular, right-aligned on the name row) and shows one "✓ Verified" chip; the full badge list stays on the c-tailor profile. (2) c-book's Continue lives in the sticky `actionBar` with "Est." as the lead (re-priced when a rush slot is picked), and the Scheduler runs a compact 3-row wheel there so the settled row is centred. (3) "Amara can't take this job" is a warning banner on both c-tailors--declined and c-tailor--declined (M1-spec updated). (4) Three-letter months everywhere ("Sep 28": `MON` 'Sept' → 'Sep'), the countdown drops a zero minutes part ("2h left"). (5) The rush ladder reads "Rush fees — ready within 1 day of the visit +$40 · 2 days +$25 · 3 days +$15 · 4+ days no rush fee", one nowrap span per tier, amounts from `t.rush`. (6) c-tailors filter pills use a pencil glyph (they open c-where, not a menu); the add-card row gets a 32 px dashed-card lead so titles align; avatar initials are sans at every size; the tailor-row skeleton matches a quoted row's height; c-code--expired explains the disabled Verify in its bar note. (7) A cold `?screen=<id>` boots that screen's PRIMARY flow state (CONTEXT.md "Cold links"). NOT done: R1-2-C-13 (collapsing identical garments into one quantity card in c-job) — a taste call left for later. Why: price is what a customer scans for; one primary action in the bar keeps the thumb path; one event, one tone. Overrule: revert the named component option (`filterPill edit`, `scheduler compact`) or the copy line.
- **D-55** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Tailor sign-in reuses `js/account.js` with a trailing `who = 'customer' | 'tailor'` on every session fn (`state.session[who]`). The seed tailor gets a phone, `(347) 555-0118`; that number is an existing tailor account, any other number is a new one. A new tailor has no name step (the Profile step asks); verifying the code calls `blankTailor()` (D-20) and stores the number. An existing tailor lands on her first incomplete step (with the "Pick up where you left off" banner), or on her shop when she is live. Why: one sign-in model and one set of error states for both personas; the Profile step already asks for her name. Overrule: give tailors a separate name step or a separate account module.
- **D-56** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Each onboarding section is one screen with two modes. The mode comes from a new tailor flag, `live`, which turns true the first time all six sections are valid and never turns back. Onboarding mode (not live) shows the status bar, a Back / Finish later bar, "Step n of 6" and Next. Edit mode (live) shows the tailor nav with Shop active, Back, and "Save changes" (disabled until something changes), then a success toast and a return to Shop. A dirty Back asks "Leave without saving?". If an edit leaves a section invalid (for example home visits turned on with no home hours), the tailor stays live but is not bookable: Shop shows a warning banner ("Customers can't book you right now", Fix it) and marks that row "Needs attention". That banner is a new state key, `t-shop--incomplete`. Why: deriving the mode from `bookable` would throw a tailor back into onboarding mid-edit, and one file per section keeps a single source for each form. Overrule: block any edit-mode save that would make the tailor unbookable.
- **D-57** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — "Finish later" saves the step exactly as typed (the section flag shows whether it is valid; no errors are shown), signs the tailor out and opens t-phone with the banner "Your progress is saved — sign in with <number> to pick up where you left off". This is a new state key, `t-phone--saved`. Signing in again resumes at the first incomplete step. Why: the prototype has no app-close, and the sign-in path shows resume through real transitions. Overrule: keep her signed in and land on an "unfinished setup" home instead.
- **D-58** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Profile: name and bio are required, the bio holds at most 300 characters (a live counter and no hard cap, so a paste can show the error), the business name and photo are optional, and up to 6 work photos are allowed. A prototype photo is `photo: true`, which `avatar` draws as the media placeholder. Why: the spec says "short bio" and "a few photos"; with no hard cap the error state stays reachable. Overrule: edit `TAILOR_LIMITS` in `js/data.js`.
- **D-59** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Tailor prices are whole dollars from $1 to $999. Under every price she types, the screen shows what she takes home after the Taily fee ("You get $22.50", from `payoutFor`). Saving services replaces the price list, so an unticked service is removed. Why: tailors quote round prices, and the take-home amount is the honest view of the fee at the moment she sets the price. Overrule: allow cents or show the fee instead of the take-home amount.
- **D-60** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Where you work: a new tailor starts with the shop on and home visits off. The shop's city and state are fixed to Brooklyn, NY (the prototype's geography), and its neighborhood comes from `ZIP_INFO`. ZIP codes are checked only for 5 digits. Turning home visits on adds the shop ZIP first. The home-visit fee is a whole dollar amount from $0 to $200, and $0 reads "No home-visit fee". Why: the seed covers Brooklyn only, and good defaults shorten the step. Overrule: add city / state fields or a real ZIP lookup.
- **D-61** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — In-store pickup needs a shop. Its row is disabled with "Needs a shop" while the shop is off, and turning the shop off in Where you work also turns pickup off. A new tailor starts with pickup on if she has a shop, drop-off on if she does home visits, and the courier off. Why: a customer can't collect from a shop that doesn't exist. Overrule: allow pickup from a home address.
- **D-62** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Availability allows one window per day for each visit type, on 30-minute steps between 9 AM and 9 PM. A new tailor starts with shop hours Tue–Sat 10 AM–6 PM and home hours Mon and Thu 6–8 PM. A day switched on copies the nearest open day above it. Shop and home windows on the same day may touch but not overlap, and both rows show the overlap error. Why: the seed uses one window per day, and the tailor can't be in two places at once. Overrule: allow several windows per day.
- **D-63** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Payouts ask for the name on the account, a routing number (9 digits) and an account number (4–17 digits). The bank name comes from a mock routing table, and only the last 4 digits are stored and shown. In edit mode, a blank account number keeps the current account. The Taily fee is explained with a new display-only constant, `MONEY.FEE_EXAMPLE_SERVICES` ($100): "On a $100.00 order, Taily keeps $10.00 and you get $90.00". The tailor's own home-visit fee and rush fees are described as "yours in full", and payouts arrive "every Friday" (D-24). Why: SCREENS.md asks for that sentence in dollars, and the base amount must live in the money config instead of being typed into a screen. Overrule: base the example on the tailor's own prices.
- **D-64** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Rush fees are not an onboarding step: a new tailor starts with none and adds them in Shop. The tiers are fixed at 1, 2 and 3 days (4+ days is free), and each fee is a whole dollar amount from $0 to $200. A later tier can't cost more than an earlier one. A $0 tier is dropped from the ladder, and switching rush fees off empties it. Why: the master plan places the rush editor in Shop and keeps onboarding at six steps; a 2-day rush costing more than a 1-day rush would confuse customers. Overrule: add rush fees as an optional step or allow custom tiers.
- **D-65** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The "What customers see" previews on to-done and t-shop are built from the demo customer's draft job (`tailorsFor(state.job)`), so the estimate shown matches Sarah's list. to-done's secondary action, "See my profile as a customer", opens c-tailor and switches the persona. c-welcome gains a line, "Are you a tailor? Join Taily" (→ t-phone), and t-phone links back ("Looking for a tailor? Book one"). Why: this shows the cross-persona link without a second demo customer, and gives the tailor entry a visible way in. Overrule: drop the links and use only the persona switch.
- **D-66** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Onboarding Test-flows setups use a new `onboardTo(step)` helper in `js/fixtures/common.js`. It resets, signs in a blank tailor with the number `(646) 555-0127`, and saves every earlier section with the seed's values through the real setters. Why: each step's state needs the steps before it done, and it must never write model data by hand. Overrule: none needed.
- **D-67** (M2, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Until t-home is built (M3), a live tailor who signs in, or who taps Go to my shop on to-done, lands on t-shop. Why: the persona home for tailors (`t-home`) is unregistered in M2. Overrule: M3 switches t-code's existing-account route to `t-home`.
- **D-68** (M2, review fixer) DEFAULTED — Back never lands on sys-index: `headingRow(inner, { back: false })` drops the Back for a nav root (t-shop, the Shop tab); t-phone wires its own Back to `back() || c-welcome`; `personaHome('tailor')` falls back to `t-shop` while `t-home` is unregistered (backstop for D-67, M3 switches it to `t-home`). t-shop's preview of a non-bookable tailor reads "What customers will see" with "Customers can't find you until <section> is finished." (same section label as the banner). to-payouts edit shows the current account as a summary (neutral-100, "Current account" caption, no stroke) followed by a "Change account" lead; the focused service price keeps its red error ring; heading-row titles use `text-wrap: balance`; t-phone's eyebrow sits inside the heading-row body. Why: R2-1-T-01..07. Overrule: revert the named component option or copy.
- **D-69** (M2, review fixer round 2) DEFAULTED — (1) Payouts: a blank account number keeps the stored last 4 only while the routing number is unchanged; a new routing number needs its account number (`setPayouts` → `account-invalid`), and the edit screen swaps the helper in place to "Enter your account number at <bank>." with the error "Enter the account number for your new bank." (2) Every "What customers see" preview uses the component's caps title (no per-screen sentence-case overrides); the tailor's own card is one shared `miniProfile` (to-profile, t-shop, to-done), and to-done with no row reads "What customers will see" plus why the sample job doesn't reach her. (3) to-returns previews the customer's own "Getting it back" rows (shared `returnsRows`, also used by c-tailor); none ticked → "No return options yet." (4) Service captions break only between whole garment names (one nowrap span each, " ·" kept on the previous name). (5) t-shop shows "Needs attention" as a second line under the summary, not in the right column. (6) Turning the shop off while in-store pickup is on shows "In-store pickup will turn off too — customers can’t collect from a shop you don’t have." and the save toast says "Where you work saved. In-store pickup is off." Why: R2-2-T-01…07 — payouts must never pair a new bank with an old account; the preview is M2's signature and must look and read the same everywhere; D-61's side effect must be visible.
- **D-70** (M2, director review; extended to the whole screen by D-98) DECIDED — One precision per money column or list (and, D-98, per screen section / card): if any amount in the group has cents, every amount in it prints with cents ("$36.00", "$18.00", "$22.50"); if none has, whole dollars. Use `fmtColumn(list)` from `js/money.js` (returns `fmt` or `fmtExact` for the group). First use: the "You get" under each price on to-services (repainted for all rows when one changes). A single standalone amount keeps `fmt`; breakdowns keep `fmtExact`. Why: R2-1-T-09 / R2-2-T-08 — a ragged "$22.50 / $36 / $18" column is hard to scan. Overrule: drop `fmtColumn` and pick one format.
- **D-71** (M2, director review) DECIDED — (1) A toast never outlives its screen: `render()` clears every toast when the screen id changes (and on a persona flip); a re-render of the same screen keeps it. A toast sits above a sticky action bar, never over its CTA. `toast(msg, { action, onAction })` adds an inline action (Undo) for 5 s. (2) The availability time wheel offers only 9 AM to 9 PM on :00 / :30: AM shows 9 / 10 / 11, PM shows 12 to 9, 9 PM shows only :00; the hour and minute rows follow the period and hour (`wireTimeWheel`), so nothing is snapped on Done. (3) to-services' action-bar lead counts exactly what the preview lists (ticked and validly priced); at 0 there is no lead, Next (or Save changes) is disabled, and the note reads "Pick at least one service to continue". (4) While the rush draft has errors, the customer preview shows the saved ladder with "Showing your saved rush fees. Fix the errors to preview your changes." (5) Work-photo tiles are at least 64 px; a tap opens a View / Remove sheet, never an instant delete, and Remove shows "Photo removed" with Undo. (6) to-done's "Add rush fees" is a link to t-rush; "See my profile as a customer" opens c-tailor in a read-only preview (no booking stepper, no action bar, "You’re viewing your own profile" + "Back to Taily for tailors"). Why: R2-2-T-10 to T-14, R2-1-T-14 and T-15. Overrule: revert the named behaviour.
- **D-72** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Detail screens (c-request, c-booking, t-request, t-job, t-thread) show the appointment named by `state.ui.apptId`, set with `setFocus(id)` by whoever navigates there; `focusAppt(screenId)` (new DOM-free `js/focus.js`) falls back to the persona's newest appointment whose `statusScreen` is that screen, and `redirectIfMoved` forwards a screen whose appointment moved on (accepted while Sarah sat on c-request). `activeAppt()` is no longer used for detail views. Why: `activeAppt()` skips declined and final-expired requests, so those outcomes had nowhere to render, and a list needs to open a specific row. Overrule: add an `apptId` route param to the router.
- **D-73** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — "You’re booked" (c-booking--confirmed) and "You accepted" (t-job--accepted) show for `POLICY.JUST_BOOKED_MIN` (60) minutes after the accept, read from the appointment history (`justConfirmed(appt)`); after that both sides show the plain upcoming layout. Why: a model-derived rule keeps both personas in step and every state reachable by moving the demo clock, with no screen-only flags. Overrule: show the celebration once per viewer (a seen-flag in `state.ui`).
- **D-74** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Before accepting, the tailor sees the customer as "Sarah C." with only neighborhood + zip; after accepting, "Sarah Chen" and the full address. Why: CORE-JOURNEY reveals the address only after accepting; the surname is the same kind of personal detail. Overrule: show the full name on the request.
- **D-75** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The t-home request card opens the request; it has no Accept button. The one-tap Accept · Suggest time · Decline live on t-request, where a push notification also lands. Why: a money decision needs the take-home figure, the work and the time in view, and one filled CTA per card list would compete. Overrule: add an Accept button to `requestCard`.
- **D-76** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Decline and can't-make-it reasons are optional single-choice chips from `DECLINE_REASONS` / `CANT_MAKE_IT_REASONS` (`js/data.js`), and the chosen reason is shown to the customer as "<Tailor>’s note". No free text in M3. Why: the doc asks for an optional reason; fixed phrases keep it quick, polite and free of contact details. Overrule: add a free-text field (masked like chat).
- **D-77** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Suggest time offers the tailor's open slots before the need-by date minus any slot that overlaps the requested visit (`suggestSlots`); `driveTo('suggested')` uses the first, Thu Oct 15 · 6:00 PM (`SUGGESTED_SLOT`). Why: a 30-minute shift inside the same visit is not a different time. Overrule: allow any other open slot.
- **D-78** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The Bookings tab has three sections: Requests (requested, suggested, expired with retries left), Upcoming (confirmed, and from M4 fitting through returning) and Past (cancelled, declined, finally expired, completed). Withdrawn requests and requests dropped for another tailor are not listed. Why: they never became bookings and the toast plus the thread already record them. Overrule: list them in Past with a "Withdrawn" pill.
- **D-79** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Destructive confirmation sheets (cancel booking, decline, can't make it, no-show, withdraw) use a new `cta` `danger` variant (error fill, white text) as their one filled button, with an outline "Keep …" button below. The confirm label states the money outcome where there is one ("Cancel and get $16.00 back", "Cancel booking ($16.00 not refunded)"). Why: the user already chose the action by opening the sheet; the red fill and the dollar amount make the consequence explicit. Overrule: make "Keep …" the filled button.
- **D-80** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — `actionBar` gains a tertiary button; with a secondary it renders as a two-column outline row under the primary (t-request: Accept full width, then Suggest time | Decline, Decline in the danger tone). Why: all three one-tap actions must be visible without scrolling, and a third full-width row would make the bar too tall. Overrule: put Decline in the page body.
- **D-81** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — M3 builds a minimal `t-thread` (key `t-thread`, re-tagged M3 like D-41): the tailor side of the same thread, with the privacy note, real sends through `T.sendMessage` and the read-only banner after a decline. The other t-thread states stay M5. Why: "Message Sarah" on the job must not be a dead end. Overrule: hide the chat entry until M5.
- **D-82** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — On t-job, "I can’t make it" is offered until the visit starts; from the start the screen says when a no-show can be marked, and "Sarah didn’t show up" appears `NO_SHOW_WAIT_MIN` (15) minutes after the start, with the pay amount from `noShowPayout`. Why: matches the model guards and keeps one honest action at each moment. Overrule: allow can't-make-it after the start.
- **D-83** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — c-tailors' none-left state reads "No other tailors for this job yet" and names the hidden tailor ("Amara didn’t reply, so Amara is hidden for this job"), with Change details and Message Amara. Overrules D-48's copy. Why: "You have seen every tailor" did not say why the list was empty. Overrule: restore D-48's strings.
- **D-84** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — t-home is the tailor's home: t-code's existing-account route and `personaHome('tailor')` go to t-home (closes D-67 / D-68's t-shop fallback). Why: t-home is now registered. Overrule: none needed.
- **D-85** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The push-denied banner's "Open Settings" simulates the user turning notifications on (`sim.push = 'granted'`, toast "Notifications are on"). Try again failures show an error banner above the button with a way out: slot passed or taken → "Pick a time" (c-book); card declined → "Pay another way" (c-pay). Why: the prototype can't open OS settings, and every error needs a next step. Overrule: make "Open Settings" a no-op toast.
- **D-86** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Multi-row list flows use a new fixture helper, `addRequest({ accept, after })` (`js/fixtures/common.js`), which books the current draft job again through the real `requestBooking` (and `tailorAccept`) at the first open slot after `after` (`SECOND_SLOT_AFTER` → Sat Oct 17 · 9:00 AM). Why: `driveTo` resets and gives one appointment; the inbox and Bookings need a request beside a booking. Overrule: seed a second customer.
- **D-87** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — One status vocabulary across personas (M3-spec §B2): Request sent / New request, New time suggested / Waiting on Sarah, Declined, No reply / Expired, Confirmed (Accepted for the first hour), Cancelled / Cancelled by Sarah / Cancelled by Amara, No-show. Both sides print the visit as `fmtWhen(slot.start)` and the same Services and Home-visit fee rows. Why: the cross-persona check must compare like with like. Overrule: edit §B2 and the pill labels.
- **D-88** (M3, spec writer) DEFAULTED — When a home visit is cancelled (by either side or as a no-show), the tailor no longer sees the street address; `jobAddress` returns neighborhood + zip again. The customer's name stays in full. Why: the address is shared only for a visit that is going ahead. Overrule: keep the address on cancelled jobs.
- **D-89** (M3, review fixer round 1) DEFAULTED — t-request reads decision-first: the take-home figure card, then the visit (time, place, need-by), then "The work", then "Your payout" (the breakdown, as on t-job). The breakdown no longer sits inside the take-home card, so the visit and the Accept bar share the first screen. Expired and declined states keep "Back to requests" in the sticky action bar, like the requested state. Why: Amara judges what, when and where in ninety seconds; the money rows repeated the headline above the fold. Overrule: move `payoutSection` back under `takeHome`. Amends M3-spec §C1 layout.
- **D-90** (M3, review fixer round 1) DEFAULTED — The Bookings list uses short customer pills: "Cancelled" for every cancel reason and "New time" for a suggestion; the detail screens keep the full §B2 words. The detail goes in the row caption: "By Amara", "Reply needed", "Try again (n of 2)". Why: the tailor's name is the row's main label and must not truncate. Overrule: restore full pill labels and shorten names instead.
- **D-91** (M3, review fixer round 1) DEFAULTED — Home waits on a suggested time separately: a request waiting on Sarah is not counted as a new request. The summary reads "No new requests · 1 waiting on Sarah" and the card sits in its own "Waiting on Sarah" section, showing the time Amara suggested (with what Sarah asked for) rather than the original. Overrule: list suggested cards under New requests.
- **D-92** (M3, review fixer round 1) DEFAULTED — The model records how and when it changed hands: `tryAgain` sets `sentAt` (c-request reads "Request sent again" with that time), `acceptSuggestion` sets `confirmedVia = 'suggestion'` (c-booking says "You accepted Amara’s new time"), `rescheduleBooking` keeps the old start as `from` on its history entry (t-job says what the visit moved from). Why: screens must not guess these from copy. Overrule: none needed.
- **D-93** (M3, review fixer round 2) DEFAULTED — One detail header for c-request, c-booking, t-request and t-job: `headingRow(hero, { backRow: true })` puts the Back alone on a 44 px row (the M1 c-tailor pattern), then the status pill, the title and the notes, all flush at the 20 px padding. c-request shows the Back row only when Sarah opened it from Bookings (Back → c-bookings); right after sending a request it has none, with the same stack. Supersedes R3-1-C-04's pill-above-Back layout on c-booking. Why: R3-2-C-02 — the left edge zig-zagged 20 / 48 / 20, and the "same ticket on both sides" had two header anatomies. Overrule: drop `backRow` and return to the inline Back.
- **D-94** (M3, review fixer round 2) DEFAULTED — A request that expired with tries left offers Try again only while the visit starts after the new reply window ends (`slot.start > now + REQUEST_EXPIRY_H`). Otherwise c-request (`c-request--expired-passed`) shows "Your <fmtWhen> time has passed" (or "is too soon for another 2-hour wait") at the padding, a filled "Pick a new time" (→ c-book with the same tailor), "Message Amara" and "See other tailors". The D-85 error banner stays for real races (slot taken, card declined). Why: R3-2-C-04 — the screen offered a primary action it already knew would fail. Overrule: keep Try again and rely on the D-85 banner.
- **D-95** (M3, review fixer round 2) DEFAULTED — The pill tone follows whose move it is. "New time suggested" / "New time" (the customer must reply) is the filled accent pill (`pill--action`: accent-ink fill, white text, the "!" glyph) and its Bookings caption "Reply needed" is accent ink, weight 600. The tailor's side of the same state, "Waiting on Sarah", is the outlined accent pill (`statusPill('waiting')`, accent-bg / accent-ink, clock glyph) on t-request and the t-home card. Passive states keep their tones. Why: R3-2-C-05 — the one row that needed Sarah was the quietest grey. Overrule: map `suggested` back to `pill--awaiting-customer`.
- **D-96** (M3, review fixer round 2) DEFAULTED — (1) `render()` scrolls to the top whenever the screen id changes; a re-render of the same screen keeps its scroll (sheets, toasts, busy repaints). (2) `fmtTime` / `fmtWhen` join the time and AM / PM with a no-break space, and the small notes on c-request and c-booking use `text-wrap: pretty`. (3) t-request's "Waiting on Sarah" state has the sticky action bar like every other state: Message Sarah (filled) and Back to requests. Why: R3-2-C-01 (c-booking opened mid-page after Accept new time), R3-2-C-03 ("5:30 / PM", orphaned last words), R3-2-T-01. Overrule: none needed.
- **D-97** (M3, Figma sync) DEFAULTED — M3 frames live in a section "M3 — Request loop" on `User - v5` (7 frames, journey order: c-request--suggested, --declined, --expired, c-booking--confirmed, c-bookings, c-booking, c-booking--cant-make-it) and on `Tailor - v5` (8 frames: t-home, t-home--empty, t-request, t-suggest, t-request--expired, t-job--accepted, t-job, t-thread) — the inventory's M3 `frame` states only; overlays (withdraw, cancel, reschedule, decline, can't-make-it / no-show sheets) and code-only states are not framed. Same build method as D-53: absolutely positioned layers from the rendered DOM (real copy and dollar amounts), every text fill / size / family and every rectangle fill / stroke / radius bound to Taily variables, master instances where the slots match one to one (CTA Default / Secondary, v5 / Money Row kinds, v5 / Avatar, v5 / Privacy Note). M1's `c-request` (824:4527) and M2's `to-profile`, `to-services`, `to-done`, `t-rush` were rebuilt in place (ids kept) because their UI changed after the M1 / M2 sync; `t-rush--invalid` has no frame, so the `t-rush` primary was re-synced instead. No binding fallbacks and no raw paints (no scrims in these states). Ids in `scripts/screens.json` → `v5.screens` / `v5.boards`. Why: the inventory marks only ◆ states as frames, and in-place rebuilds keep links and comments on the earlier frames. Overrule: frame the M3 overlays as siblings with the screen behind them.
- **D-98** (M3, review fixer round 3) DEFAULTED — (1) **One figure, one precision per screen** (extends D-70 from "column" to "screen"): if any amount on a screen prints with cents, every amount of the same kind there does too. `workList` (t-request, t-job) prints its lines and estimate with `fmtExact` because the payout breakdown beside it is exact; c-tailor, c-tailors and c-bookings compute one `fmtColumn(allAmounts)` for the screen (`priceRow` and `tailorRow` take a `format`). (2) **System lines are written from the reader's side**: `say(appt, customerText, tailorText)`; the customer reads "You sent a request" / "Amara accepted" / "You cancelled the booking", the tailor reads "Sarah sent a request" / "You accepted" / "You can’t make it"; t-thread prints `m.tailorText`. (3) **Past rows say what happened to the money** (supersedes the "By Amara" caption of D-90): amount = the deposit, caption "Refunded" / "Deposit kept" / "Hold released"; who ended it moves into the place line ("You cancelled", "Amara cancelled"); a past row's pill is neutral (`pill--calm` look) unless money was kept or the tailor ended it (declined, can’t make it, late cancel, no-show: `appt-row--kept` keeps the error tone). Sarah’s early cancel and a plain "No reply" use the neutral `calm` pill on the detail screens too. (4) c-tailors counts only bookable (not declined) rows; with none: "No tailors available for this job". none-left copy: "<Amara> didn’t reply in time. We’ll let you know when more tailors join near <zip>." with Change need-by date / Change ZIP (overrules D-83's copy and its Message CTA). (5) c-request--suggested keeps the "At my place · 60 min" row. (6) The reschedule sheet shows "Now: <current time>" above the new one. (7) `.back` hit target is 45×44 (::after inset 4 / 11). (8) A garment's photos are a tappable 44 px thumbnail + count (`openPhotoViewer`, placeholder images — the prototype has no photo files) on t-request / t-job. (9) t-home hides the "0" count on an empty section. (10) t-job's no-focus fallback names waiting requests ("You have 1 request to answer on Home."); t-request's has the same Back heading. (11) Fixture clock moves to the exact expiry (`driveTo('expired*')`: +120 min, not +121) so deadlines read on :00. Why: R3-1-T-11, R3-2-T-02, R3-1/2-T-05/12, R3-1-C-12/13/16/14/15/10/C-06/07/08/09, R3-2-T-07a/08/09/10/11. Overrule: revert to fmt in workList and a single system text.
- **D-99** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The return method is chosen in two steps. When Amara marks the order Ready, a sheet lists the return options she offers in Shop, all ticked; she can untick any for this order (at least one stays). Sarah then picks one of those on c-ready and pays. Model: `markReady(id, { returns })` stores `appt.returnOffer` (default: every option the tailor offers); `chooseReturn` refuses an option outside it (`not-offered`); new selector `returnOffer(appt)`. Why: CORE-JOURNEY gives the tailor "pick the return method" and the customer "picks a return option the tailor offers"; a per-order offer honours both (a bridal gown might be pickup only) and makes `c-ready--pickup-only` reachable through a real transition. Overrule: drop the sheet's checkboxes and always offer every Shop option.
- **D-100** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Courier money is printed in dollars as three parts on both sides: Uber quote + Taily courier fee = courier total (`courierQuote(miles)` now returns `{ miles, quote, markup, cents }`; `cents` is unchanged). The customer sees them on c-ready and on the courier booking; the tailor sees them only in an info card on the courier-booked job, captioned "Paid by Sarah. Not taken from your payout." The courier never enters `tailorBreakdown` or the payout. Partially overrules D-04 (the courier now appears on the tailor's side, outside her money). Why: the milestone brief asks the tailor to see the booked quote and markup, and it must be clear she does not pay it. Overrule: hide the card on t-job (restore D-04 in full).
- **D-101** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — A price change can carry an optional note to the customer (at most `TAILOR_LIMITS.NOTE_MAX` 200 characters; phone numbers and emails are masked as in chat). While the change is pending or Sarah has messaged about it, Amara can revise it from t-job ("Change the new price" → t-adjust prefilled with the pending lines). Sending again replaces the pending change, sets it back to pending and posts "Amara changed the updated price" to the thread. The added / removed / re-priced marks always compare with the booked lines. Model: `sendPriceChange(id, lines, { note })` also accepts status `price-change` with `priceChange.state` pending | messaged. Why: §2 says disputes settle in chat; a chat that ends in "I'll take the sleeves back off" needs a way to resend, and the note says why the price moved. Overrule: one price change per job, no note.
- **D-102** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Adjusting a price (t-adjust): prices are whole dollars from $1 to `TAILOR_LIMITS.PRICE_MAX` (as D-59); a service can be added only from Amara's own price list and only where it fits the garment type, starting at her list price; any line can be removed (with Undo) but at least one service must remain ("Keep at least one service on this job."); Send is enabled once anything differs from the starting price, and price errors show at Send, next to the field. The draft lives in `state.ui['t-adjust'] = { apptId, mode: 'new'|'revise', prices: { [garmentId:serviceId]: '50' }, removed: [key], added: [{ garmentId, serviceId }], note, tried }`. Why: the price list is her contract with every customer; errors belong at submit (D-45); Test-flows seed the draft to stand in for typing (D-34). Overrule: allow free-text services or cents.
- **D-103** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — From the visit start, t-job shows the job's next step in a sticky action bar and "Message Sarah" drops to the secondary button: Start fitting (confirmed, from the start time; the model refuses earlier) → **No changes, start the work** (primary) or Adjust price (secondary) → Mark ready (sheet, D-99) → Mark completed. Mark completed has no confirm sheet: the label states the act, it shows busy, and the toast and the screen give the payout and its date. A completed job leaves Home's Upcoming list (M5 Earnings lists it); the toast is the receipt. Before the start, t-job is unchanged from M3. Why: one obvious next action at each step, reachable with a thumb; a confirm on the routine last step would add a tap without adding safety (the order is in Sarah's hands). Overrule: add a confirm sheet before Mark completed.
- **D-104** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Paying the balance: c-ready's pay sheet lists Sarah's saved cards and Apple Pay (default card first); adding a card waits for M5 (c-cards). There is no 3-D Secure step on the balance (the card was verified when the deposit was held), so the screen calls `payBalance(id, cardId, { threeDS: true })`. A decline closes the sheet and shows an error banner on c-ready, read from `payments.balance.state === 'failed'` ("Nothing was charged"); picking another method clears the decline (`useOtherMethod`, D-40). Paying shows the busy CTA ("Paying…") with the methods locked — a new state key `c-ready--processing`. Why: the balance is a second charge on a known card; the error must stay visible after the sheet closes. Overrule: run 3-D Secure again on the balance.
- **D-105** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The courier is priced the moment Sarah picks it: the option calls `chooseReturn(id, 'courier')` through `withLatency` and shows "Getting a price from Uber…" while the request runs or while `appt.courier.state` is `loading` (the `sim.courier` knob). A failed quote shows "Couldn’t get a courier price" with Try again, which simulates the courier service recovering (sets `sim.courier = 'ok'`, like D-85) and asks again. The other options stay selectable throughout. When the order offers a single option, it renders already selected and Pay chooses it. Why: the price must be known before Sarah pays, and a failure must never trap her. Overrule: make Try again keep failing until the knob is reset.
- **D-106** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — After the balance is paid, each return reads differently on both sides: in-store pickup → "Ready for pickup" (shop name and address); home drop-off → "Return scheduled" on the customer side and "To drop off" on the tailor side, with the time agreed in chat (the model has no drop-off time); courier → "Out for delivery" for the customer and "Courier booked" for the tailor, collected from the shop and delivered today. Drop-off and courier always deliver to Sarah's saved address (also for shop visits). The tailor marks the job Completed once it is handed over. Why: there is no scheduling model for returns, and chat already joins the two people. Overrule: add a drop-off time picker.
- **D-107** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Reviews: 1–5 stars required ("Choose a star rating."), text optional up to `REVIEW_LIMITS.TEXT_MAX` 500 characters, up to `REVIEW_LIMITS.PHOTOS_MAX` 3 placeholder photos (as D-42). Phone numbers and emails in the text are masked before it goes public (`leaveReview` uses `maskContact`). A posted review is final in the prototype. It appears first on c-tailor as "Sarah C." with the average and count updated, and the tailor sees it on the completed job. Tapping a star on c-complete opens c-review with that rating already chosen. New state keys `c-tailor--reviewed`, `c-booking--completed` (a reviewed order opens c-booking), `c-review--photos`. Why: §10 fixes the review shape; a public review must not leak contact details. Overrule: allow editing a review.
- **D-108** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — M4 status words (M4-spec §B2) keep D-95's rule that the pill tone follows whose move it is. A new generic `statusPill('action', label)` key uses the accent-filled `pill--action`. Customer: price change → action "Approve new price" (list: "New price" + "Reply needed"); ready → action "Ready" (list caption "Pay now"). Tailor: price change → `waiting` "Waiting on Sarah"; Sarah messaged → action "Reply to Sarah"; ready → `waiting` "Waiting on Sarah"; drop-off → action "To drop off". A DOM-free `statusWord(appt, persona, { short })` in `js/focus.js` returns `{ pill, label }` for every M4 status, so lists and details print the same word. Why: the cross-persona check needs one vocabulary, and the person who must act needs the loudest pill. Overrule: edit §B2 and `statusWord`.
- **D-109** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — SCREENS.md corrections: `c-price-change--repriced` is Sleeves $45 → $50 with no rows added or removed (driveTo's `repriced` recipe; the total goes up — the lower-balance case is `--removed`); `SCENARIOS` gains `priceRepriced` and `courierQuote`. New M4 state keys: `c-price-change--messaged` ·, `c-ready--processing` O, `c-booking--completed` ·, `c-bookings--ready` ·, `c-tailor--reviewed` ·, `c-review--photos` ·, `t-job--visit` ·, `t-job--mark-ready` O, `t-adjust--add` O, `t-adjust--revise` ·, `t-adjust--leave` O. Why: every state the spec renders needs a Test-flows key and a baseline. Overrule: drop the extra keys.
- **D-110** (M4, spec writer) DEFAULTED — c-booking's "What happens next" grows to five steps — Booked · Visit and fitting · Tailoring · Ready · Back with you — each finished step stamped with what happened in dollars where there is money ("Price confirmed · $160.00", "Paid $144.00"). Booking list amounts follow the price: "Est. $160" until the fitting confirms it, then the total; ready → the balance with "Pay now"; returning and completed → the total with "Paid"; a completed order without a review → "Leave a review". Why: the timeline is the customer's view of the ticket moving through the shop; the list must not keep saying "Est." after the price is settled. Overrule: keep the four-step M3 timeline.
- **D-111** (M4, component builder) DEFAULTED — `changeList` tags ("Added", "New price", "Removed") are 10 / 600 with the caps tracking but stay sentence case (no `text-transform`). Why: the flow harness reads `innerText`, so an uppercase transform would turn "Added" into "ADDED" and break every `has` / `not` check (and a `not: 'Added'` would pass for the wrong reason); the pill outline still marks it as a tag. `deltaFigure`'s label is a real `.t-caps` (uppercase in `innerText`; fixtures match it case-insensitively or by `sel`). Also: `courierQuote` is the single source of the courier split (`quote + markup === cents`), `chooseReturn` refuses an option that is not in `returnOffer(a)` AND one the tailor has since switched off in Shop, `statusPill('action')` reuses the existing `pill--action` rule. Overrule: add `text-transform: uppercase` to `.change-list__tag` and have fixtures match the caps.
- **D-112** (M4, review fixer round 1) DEFAULTED — Whose move a messaged price change is follows the chat: `lastSpeakerSince(appt, sentAt)` (state.js, DOM-free) returns the latest customer / tailor message from the price change on. While Sarah spoke last, Amara's side reads "Reply to Sarah" (action pill, "Sarah wants to talk about the price", filled Reply CTA, Home word "Reply"). Once Amara has answered, it reads "Waiting on Sarah" (outlined pill, Home word "Waiting"), title "Waiting for Sarah to approve", note "You replied at <time>. Sarah can approve now, or you can change the price.", Message Sarah primary and "Change the new price" secondary (the waiting layout). New flow `t-job--replied`. Amended by R4-2-T-02: every "waiting on Sarah" case (price change pending or replied, ready, suggested) prints the short "Waiting" in lists and "Waiting on Sarah" on the detail screens; the shared `waiting` word in `statusWord` now honours `short` (t-home--suggested re-baselined if it moves). Why: D-95 / D-108 say the pill tone follows whose move it is; after her reply the app was still nagging Amara. Overrule: key the word on `priceChange.state` alone.
- **D-113** (M4, review fixer round 1) DEFAULTED — (1) c-booking's timeline Ready step says "Pay the balance, est. $X" only while the price is unconfirmed (confirmed, fitting, price-change); from in-progress on it reads "Pay the $X balance", since a confirmed figure is never an estimate. M4-spec §C4's timeline line is amended to match. (2) c-ready with exactly one way back offered is not a choice: the heading is "Getting it back" and the hero reads "<First> finished the work. Pay the balance, then pick it up at <shop> / <First> brings it to your door / a courier brings it today." The "offers … for this order" line stays. (3) Item and service counts on c-booking and the tailor's request view come from the approved `lines`, not the booked job, so they follow a price change. (4) t-job's "The work" total reads "Services total" instead of "Estimate from your prices" once the work has started (in-progress and later); "Estimate" stays for confirmed, visit, fitting. Overrule: restore the earlier strings.
- **D-114** (M4, review fixer round 1) DEFAULTED — "See Amara's profile" from a finished review opens c-tailor in profile mode (`state.ui.booking = { tailorId, from: 'review' }`, cleared by every booking-path screen that re-picks a tailor): Bookings tab, no booking stepper, no draft estimate, and one action, "Book Amara again" (new job, then c-job). Back returns to c-review. Why: a finished job's profile must not look like a booking in progress. Overrule: open the booking-path profile.
- **D-115** (M4, review fixer round 1) DEFAULTED — t-home is first-run only when the tailor has no appointments at all. Otherwise it shows "No new requests right now", sections "Upcoming" (confirmed, before the visit starts) and "In progress" (the visit and every step after, rows read "<step> · since <time>" instead of the past visit time; the summary says "1 job in progress"), and a "Recent" section with the latest completed job as a muted row (Completed pill, payout, "Pays out / Paid out <day>"). Home tab opens t-job--completed from that row. Also: `fmtNeg(0)` prints "$0.00" (never "−$0.00"), and t-adjust holds its figures at the start values with "Add a service back to see the new price" while every service is removed. Pickup return on c-booking shows the shop's next open hours under the pickup block, followed by the message-Amara note. Overrule: none needed.

- **D-116** (M4, review fixer round 2) DEFAULTED — c-bookings splits out an **In progress** section (fitting, price change, in progress, ready, returning) between Requests and Upcoming, matching the tailor's Home (D-115). Its rows read the step and since when instead of the past visit time: "Fitting / New price / Tailoring / Ready / Ready for pickup / Return scheduled / Out for delivery · since 5:00 PM" (today) or "· since Thu, Oct 15". Upcoming keeps only confirmed visits. Both personas take the time from one DOM-free helper, `sinceText(appt)` in `js/focus.js` (the latest history entry for the current status). The list row's time line wraps to a second line instead of ending in an ellipsis (`.appt-row__when`). Why: R4-2-C-05 / R4-2-T-01 — "Upcoming" was wrong for work that had started, the two personas disagreed, and the time was the part cut off. Overrule: fold In progress back into Upcoming.
- **D-117** (M4, review fixer round 2) DEFAULTED — While a price change waits on Sarah (pending or messaged), the tailor's Home row shows the take-home **if she approves** — `priceChangeView(a).after.payout` — captioned "If Sarah approves", the same figure t-job--waiting leads with ("You'll take home", "Your payout if approved"). Once approved it is the booked payout again with "You take home". Why: R4-2-T-03 — Home and the job it opens printed two different take-homes for one job. Overrule: keep the booked figure on Home with the caption "Until Sarah approves".
- **D-118** (M4, review fixer round 2) DEFAULTED — Payment and price-change honesty fixes: (1) c-ready treats a courier quote in flight as the choice — the courier row is checked, the skeleton and "Getting a price…" show, and Pay stays disabled ("Waiting for the courier price.") until the price lands; the pay sheet refuses while quoting. (2) The pay sheet cannot be dismissed while "Paying…" (✕ disabled, scrim and Escape ignored: `holder.dataset.locked`, honoured by `sheetOverlay`); if the router removes the screen mid-payment anyway, the charge still completes and a toast says the result. (3) After a decline, `payBalance` records `payments.balance.failedCardId`; "Pay another way" opens the sheet with the next method selected (Apple Pay) and the declined card's row reads "Expires 08/28 · Declined just now" in error ink; paying with the other method clears the decline (D-40). (4) Opening the chat from c-price-change changes nothing; the change becomes "messaged" on Sarah's first `sendMessage` after it was sent (state.js), so the "You messaged Amara" banner, "Open chat" and Amara's "Reply to Sarah" are always backed by a real message. (5) One return-address line on both sides, `returnAddress()` (state.js): street + unit ("145 N 7th St, 2R") for the courier route and the drop-off block on c-ready, c-booking and t-job; only the visit block adds the city. (6) t-job: every live state ends "Your payout" with "Paid out the Friday after you mark the job completed." ("The price can change after the fitting." only at visit and fitting; "Price updated after the fitting." once approved); the completed note names the customer ("Sarah Chen’s order, completed …"); the completed payout is one card with the breakdown inside; the courier state reads "A courier picks it up today" / "…ready at Reyes Tailoring today…"; return blocks read place name (w-600, beside its icon) then the address in regular weight on the same text edge. (7) `deltaFigure` prints "Same as booked" when the figure has not moved, never "Was $X · no change". Why: R4-2-C-01…C-04, C-06, R4-2-T-04…T-09. Overrule: none needed.
- **D-119** (M4, Figma sync) DEFAULTED — M4 frames live in a section "M4 — Fitting to completion" on `User - v5` (5 frames, journey order: c-price-change, c-ready, c-complete, c-review, c-review--submitted) and on `Tailor - v5` (2 frames: t-adjust, t-job--completed) — the inventory's M4 ◆ states only; c-booking's M4 states, t-job's M4 states other than --completed, and the overlays (c-ready--pay) are code-only and not framed. Same build method as D-53 / D-97 (absolutely positioned layers from the rendered DOM at the real copy and dollar amounts; text fill / size / family and rectangle fill / stroke / radius bound to Taily variables; CTA, v5 / Money Row, v5 / Avatar master instances where the slots match), plus struck text (re-priced / removed amounts in the change list) kept as Figma STRIKETHROUGH, and the status-bar / logo icons cloned from an existing v5 frame instead of re-imported. The M3 frames t-request, t-job, c-bookings, c-request--suggested were rebuilt in place (ids kept). `c-tailors--none-left` had no frame (it is a code-only state of an M1 screen), so it was framed new as the last frame of the M3 section on `User - v5` (it is the end of the request loop). The M4 components changeList / deltaFigure / courierCard / pill--action have no Figma masters yet, so they are drawn with bound tokens. No binding fallbacks, no raw paints; the ★ glyphs fall back from Hanken Grotesk at render. Ids in `scripts/screens.json` → `v5.screens` / `v5.boards`. Why: matches the M1–M3 sync so reviewers see one consistent file. Overrule: add v5 masters for the M4 components and swap them in.

- **K-01** (Kevin, Oct 2026) LOCKED — At the appointment the tailor edits the job itself, as in v4 (t04/t05 → 04 Review & Approve): add/remove **items (garments)** and add/remove/re-price **services**, then **send the final order to the customer for approval to begin tailoring**. Tailoring starts only after the customer approves. Director call (D-K01a): the approval step is ALWAYS required after the fitting, even with no changes. An unchanged order reaches Sarah as "No changes — approve to start" (one tap). Changed rows use v4's NEW / removed / re-priced marking (success colour for added, struck for removed).
- **D-120** (K-01, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The fitting always ends in Sarah's approval. The model keeps the `price-change` status id (the 12-status model's "waiting for the customer's approval") and `sendPriceChange` now means "send the final order": it accepts an unchanged order and stores `priceChange.changed` (false when nothing moved). `confirmNoChange` is deleted from `state.js` and `T`, and D-103's "No changes, start the work" path is gone: t-job--fitting's one primary is "Review the order", Start fitting lands straight on the editor, and t-adjust's one primary is "Send to Sarah for approval", enabled with zero edits (bar lead "No changes · You take home $X"). `driveTo` targets past the fitting send the unchanged order and approve it. Every "price updated after the fitting" claim (t-job captions, the approved banner, c-booking's "Updated after the fitting") is gated on `priceChange.changed`. Why: K-01 (D-K01a) makes approval required even with no changes, and a single gate means the model can't be walked around it. Overrule: keep `confirmNoChange` as a tailor-only shortcut for unchanged orders.
- **D-121** (K-01, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Item edits at the fitting: a new item is just lines on a new `garmentId` (`n1`, `n2`, … in draft order); removing an item means sending none of its lines. The editor always diffs against the BOOKED order, so a revise draft is rebuilt from the sent order and its marks match Sarah's. An item is created only through a two-step sheet (type → service), so it is never empty. The type list shows only types Amara prices at least one service for, with "From $X". Up to `MAX_ITEMS` (10) items. Removing a booked item strikes its card in place with Undo, and its services are listed struck. Removing a new item drops it with an Undo toast. Removing the last service of an item removes the item. On approval `job.garments` is rebuilt from the approved lines (new items get `photos: 0, added: true`). `sendPriceChange` validates `no-items`, `price-invalid` (whole dollars $1–$999), `garment-type`, `service-type`, `service-not-offered`, `duplicate-line` and `max-items`. Why: v4's t04 editor let the tailor add garments and services at the visit; nothing is sent until Send, so an inline Undo is safer and faster than a confirm modal (as D-102 does for services). Overrule: a confirm sheet before removing a booked item.
- **D-122** (K-01, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The marking follows v4's NEW badge in v5 type. Added services are tagged "New" (was "Added") and added items "New item", in a filled `--success` badge with white text (4.9:1) on a `--success-bg` tint. A new item is one green panel with no per-row tags. A removed item is struck with one "Removed" tag. Re-priced keeps M4's struck old amount and "New price". Tags stay sentence case (D-111). A new `changeSummary(counts)` line ("1 item added · 1 service removed", or "No changes. Same items, same price as booked.") heads the list on both sides. Why: Kevin asked for v4's green NEW marking, and the summary lets Sarah see the scope before reading rows. The accent colour stays reserved for "your move" (D-95). Overrule: keep the accent "Added" tag.
- **D-123** (K-01, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Vocabulary for the final order. Tailor: t-adjust "AT THE FITTING / Edit the job" (revise "Edit the order you sent", "Send the updated order"); t-job "The order you sent", "Edit the order", "Sarah approved your changes" (changed) or "Sarah approved the order" (as booked); t-home step "Order sent". Customer: c-price-change "Review your final order", pill "Approve order" (list "Approve"), CTA "Approve and start", "Total" / "Balance" (without "New") when unchanged; c-bookings step "Final order"; c-booking timeline "Order approved · $X". Thread lines: "Amara sent your final order" / "You sent the final order", "Amara updated your final order", "You approved the order" / "Sarah approved the order". Why: "price" was wrong once items can be added or nothing can change, and v4's "Approve your final order" is the phrase Kevin liked. Overrule: keep the M4 price words for the changed case.
- **D-124** (K-01, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Money with item edits: the deposit stays as captured even when items are added (D-23, MASTER-PLAN §2); the home-visit fee and rush are unchanged; only services, the Taily fee, the total, the balance and the payout move, all from `quote()`. New scenarios: `SCENARIOS.priceItem` (J6: + Pants / Jeans · Hem pants, − Take in jacket sides → total $130.00, balance $114.00, payout $120.50) and `SCENARIOS.priceItemRemoved` (− the Suit Pant → total $135.00, payout $125.00); driveTo recipes `item`, `item-removed`, `unchanged`. Why: one money model on both sides; fixtures never type dollars. Overrule: re-take the deposit on a larger order.
- **D-125** (K-01, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The M4 journeys J6–J8 in M4-spec §D4 were never added to `scripts/v5/journeys.mjs` (only J1–J5 exist). K-01 adds J6 (Amara adds a Pants / Jeans item and removes a service, Sarah approves, tailoring starts, both sides read the same ticket) and J6b (no changes still needs Sarah's tap). J7 (courier) and J8 (completed → review) are still owed and logged in PROGRESS.md. Why: the K-01 loop is cross-persona and needs a journey; the gap was found while specifying it. Overrule: none needed. J7/J8 landed in M5 (D-143).
- **D-126** (K-01, fixer, K-1-02) DEFAULTED — t-adjust action-bar lead shows both live figures, the customer's first: "Sarah pays $130.00 · You take home $120.50", or "No changes · Sarah pays $160.00 · You take home $147.50" when untouched (wraps to two lines at 390 if needed; each pair stays unbroken). Amends K01-spec §C1's bar lead. Why: at the table Sarah watches the total move while Amara edits the cards; the only live figure was Amara's payout, and Sarah's total sat ~1,300 px down in "What changes". The lower block stays as the detailed breakdown. Also (K-1-01): struck old amounts (`.change-list__was`, removed-row amount, `.t-adjust__was`, `.t-adjust__mark s`) use `--neutral-600` (about 6:1) instead of `--neutral-500` (3.7:1 on the page), meeting the 4.5:1 floor in MASTER-PLAN §10.6. Overrule: keep Amara's payout as the only bar figure.
- **D-127** (K-01, fixer, K-2-01) DEFAULTED — The customer sees the approved order.
