From in-progress through completed, c-booking has a "Your order" section after "What happens next" (after "Your review" on a completed booking, before the Receipt), and c-ready shows the same section above Price, so the balance she pays is backed by a line list. Both are built from the approved `a.lines` with `changeList({ garments: priceChangeView(a, a.lines).garments })`: once approved every row is 'same', so they print as plain rows, all `fmtExact`, with the same garment and service strings as Amara's "The work" on t-job. A garment added at the fitting (`job.garments[i].added`) keeps a small "New item" tag on its type line (new `changeList` garment flag `newItem`, no green panel); removed items and services are simply absent. Before the fitting (confirmed, fitting) and while the order waits on her (c-price-change) nothing changes. Why: c-booking said "3 items · 3 services" and "Order approved · $130.00" with no list, while t-job listed the work; both sides now read the same ticket.
- **D-128** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — One thread per customer–tailor pair (kept keyed by tailor id; the demo has one customer). Unread is tracked per reader as the highest message number seen (`thread.read = { customer, tailor }`); only the other person's messages count as unread, system lines never do, your own send is never unread, and opening a thread marks it read. Messages lists sort by latest activity and preview from the reader's point of view ("You: …", "Photo", "phone hidden"). No unread dot on the Messages tab in M5 (it would re-baseline every screen with an unread line). Both thread screens render messages through one shared `threadMessages` component with day dividers. Why: CORE-JOURNEY's one-thread rule needs a findable, honest inbox, and one renderer keeps the two sides identical. Overrule: add the tab dot in M6 and re-accept the baselines.
- **D-129** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Every thread carries a context row under its header ("the thread is pinned to the ticket"): the thread's appointment (`threadAppt`: the pair's active one, else the newest that wasn't withdrawn or switched, else none) with its short status word, visit time and a link to its status screen. While a request is pending the header shows the countdown ("Request sent · 2h left" / "New request · 2h left to reply"). Before any booking the customer's row reads "No booking yet" and opens Amara's profile; the tailor's is static ("Sarah is asking before booking"). The tailor sees "Sarah C." in threads until any appointment of the pair was accepted (D-74). Why: CORE-JOURNEY puts the countdown in the chat; a message is only useful with its job in view. Overrule: drop the row and keep the header line only.
- **D-130** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Sending is async ("Sending…" under the bubble). A failed send stays on the sender's device only (`state.ui`), shows "Not sent · Retry" and a "Message not sent" toast. `sim.send = 'fail'` fails the NEXT send only and resets itself (a dropped connection), so Retry succeeds; `sim.network = 'offline'` fails every send until the offline banner's Retry. While the draft contains a phone number or email a live hint above the composer says "Phone numbers and emails are hidden when you send."; the sent bubble shows the standing privacy note to the sender only; both sides see the hidden part as a "phone hidden" / "email hidden" token. Why: the rule must be visible before the user is surprised, and the demo must be able to recover from a failure. Overrule: keep `sim.send` sticky and remove the hint.
- **D-131** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Photos in chat: up to `CHAT_LIMITS.PHOTOS_MAX` 3 per message, attached from a sheet (Take a photo / Choose from library — placeholders, as D-42), shown in a removable tray above the input; a photo-only message is allowed; tapping a photo opens the shared photo viewer. Why: CORE-JOURNEY allows garment photos; three matches reviews (D-107). Overrule: change `CHAT_LIMITS`.
- **D-132** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — After a decline the customer's thread is read-only with "Amara declined this request, so this chat is closed. Send Amara a new request for a different job to chat again.", primary "Find another tailor" (job kept) and secondary "Start a different job with Amara" (`newJob` → c-job). The same thread reopens when she sends Amara a new request (`threadReadOnly` already keys on the latest appointment). The tailor reads "You declined this request, so the conversation is closed. Sarah can send you a new request for a different job." Why: CORE-JOURNEY — read-only, Find another tailor, and the tailor can be requested again for a different job. Overrule: allow messaging a declined tailor.
- **D-133** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Account profile (name, phone, email) is read-only in the prototype ("You sign in with this number."). Saved addresses are a list with one default (`customer.addresses`; `customer.address` mirrors the default or is null); add in a sheet (label Home / Work / Other, street, unit, ZIP; city and state from `SHOP_CITY`, "Taily is in Brooklyn for now."); remove is immediate with an Undo toast, no confirm; removing the default promotes the next; new jobs and returns start from the default; changing it never moves an existing booking. Why: SCREENS lists no edit-profile screen; an address is cheap to restore, so Undo beats a confirm. Overrule: add a profile edit sheet; confirm address removal.
- **D-134** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Payment methods: tapping a card opens one sheet with Make default and Remove card; Remove swaps the same sheet to a confirm (danger "Remove card" + "Keep card") that names what moves ("Your booking with Amara on Thu, Oct 15 · 5:00 PM will use Mastercard •••• 4444 instead.", "… becomes your default."). A card paying for an open booking can be removed only when another card exists — the booking moves to the new default; if it is the only card, Remove is replaced by "Add another card first" (`removeCard` → `in-use`). Apple Pay is listed as a non-removable "Set up in Wallet on this phone" row. Why: a booking must never lose its way to pay the balance (MASTER-PLAN §2 balance at Ready). Overrule: block removal of any card in use. M6 (P-16): the confirm sheet header reads "Remove card" and names what moves with the balance in dollars: "The $144.00 balance for your booking with Amara on Thu, Oct 15 · 5:00 PM will be charged to Mastercard •••• 4444 instead." (the booking is paid at Ready, so the balance — not the booking — is what changes card).
- **D-135** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Past orders = bookings that were accepted and then completed or cancelled (incl. no-show); requests never accepted aren't orders. Amounts in words: completed → total "Paid"; cancelled early or by the tailor → "Refunded"; late or no-show → "Kept". A row opens the booking (its Receipt) or c-complete. Why: "orders + receipts" means money that moved. Overrule: list every request.
- **D-136** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Payouts settle in an explicit weekly run, `settlePayouts()` (fixtures call it; clock moves never do): pending payouts due by today become paid (bank snapshotted) or, with `sim.payout = 'failed'`, failed. Saving bank details (`setPayouts`) re-queues failed payouts for the next Friday ("Sending again Fri, Oct 23"). Earnings shows the next Friday's total as the one figure, its jobs under it, then Paid groups by Friday; a failed payout is an error banner with the amount, bank, retry date and "Fix bank details" (→ to-payouts). Every amount is after the Taily fee and says so. `payoutDay(iso)` (dates.js) replaces the private `payoutDate`. Why: D-24 gives weekly Friday payouts; T8 needs pending / paid / failed reachable. Overrule: pay on completion.
- **D-137** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The per-job payout (t-payout) lists the Taily fee right under Services with "On the $125.00 of services only" (`tailorBreakdown(q, { feeAfterServices: true, feeCaption })`), then the home-visit and rush fees, then Your payout; t-job / t-request keep the M3 order. Kept deposits and no-show pay show one row and "No Taily fee on a kept deposit." The courier is never in the payout (D-04) and a courier job says so. Why: the fee's base is services only, which reads plainly when the fee sits next to it. Overrule: use the default order everywhere.
- **D-138** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — System layer (new `js/system.js`, wired by the router): an offline banner under the top bar on every screen of both personas while `sim.network = 'offline'` ("You’re offline. Some things won’t load or send until you’re back." + Retry → "Checking…" → reconnects, "You’re back online"). `sim.session = 'expired'` opens a locked re-auth sheet on any signed-in screen: a code is sent to the account's own number, Verify, Resend, and Sign out as the only exit (sign-in screens exempt). Why: offline and expired sessions are global; one layer keeps every host unchanged. Overrule: per-screen banners.
- **D-139** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Push permission uses a soft ask (modal: benefit first, "Turn on notifications" / "Not now") before the OS prompt. Tailor: on t-home at first launch (to-done's Go to Home sets `sim.push = 'prompt'` when it is still 'default'). Customer: on c-request ("Know when Amara replies"), reachable from Test flows only (`sys-push-prompt-customer`), because a prompt after every first request would interrupt the M1–M4 journeys. "Not now" counts as denied: the tailor sees the existing banner (D-85), the customer one line under the countdown. Why: ask at the moment of value; keep existing journeys stable. Overrule: prompt the customer after her first request and update the journeys.
- **D-140** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — One error pattern everywhere (M5-spec §B3): field errors under the field; a failed action shows an error banner directly above that action saying what happened and what did not happen with money, with the way forward; a toast echoes the result but never carries an error alone. Demo knob `sim.server = 'error'` fails the next `requestBooking` once (`server-error`, nothing held) — the `sys-error-toast` state on c-pay. Why: SCREENS' "toast + inline error pattern" needs a reachable trigger that is not a card decline. Overrule: none needed.
- **D-141** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The M5 lists (both Messages tabs, Past orders, Earnings) show skeleton rows on their first load in a session (a `withLatency` wait, remembered in `state.ui[id].loaded`); new keys `c-messages--loading` and `t-earnings--loading`. Why: MASTER-PLAN §3 asks for loading skeletons on lists; one per persona is enough to baseline. Overrule: no simulated load.

- **D-143** (M5, spec writer) DEFAULTED — M5 adds journeys J7 (courier return, the three courier amounts identical on both sides and never in the payout), J8 (completed → review → c-tailor shows "Sarah C." and the count + 1; the tailor's stars are asserted by `aria-label`, not text) and J9 (Sarah asks before booking, Amara replies, the same thread continues after the request, a typed phone number is masked on both sides, the accept line reads from each side). Closes D-125. Why: the messaging rules are cross-persona and only a journey proves them. Overrule: none needed.
- **D-144** (M5, C4 screen builder) DEFAULTED — (1) to-done has no "Go to Home"; its one exit is "Go to my shop", so that tap sets `sim.push = 'prompt'` (when still `'default'`) and the soft notification ask opens the first time she lands on t-home. (2) Earnings prints every amount with `fmtExact` (the next-payout figure is always exact, so rows are too: one precision per screen). (3) A failed payout also lists its jobs under a "Didn’t go through" heading on t-earnings so each job stays reachable (→ t-payout, which shows its own banner and the sticky "Fix bank details"; the banner there carries no second button). (4) c-pay's `generic` banner copy is now "Something went wrong on our side. Nothing was held. Try again." (the server-error path; no other path set `generic` before). Extra FLOWS keys: `t-payout--no-show`, `t-payout--failed`. Why: each gap needed a reachable state and one honest way forward. Overrule: none needed (numbered D-144 by the M5 integrator).
- **D-145** (M5, integrator) DEFAULTED — The M5 model layer the screen builders imported was missing from the working tree, so the integrator wrote it from M5-spec §A1–A7 and A10 (`data.js` `CHAT_LIMITS` `ADDRESS_LABELS` `PAYOUT_KIND_LABEL` `CUSTOMER.addresses`; `dates.js` `payoutDay`; `state.js` sim knobs `payout` / `server`, `thread.read`, `markThreadRead`, `settlePayouts`, `threadAppt` `unreadCount` `threadPreview` `threadsFor`, the `sendMessage` guard order, `requestBooking` `server-error`, the `setPayouts` re-queue; `tailor-data.js` `threadCustomerName` `payoutsView` `payoutDetail`; `account.js` addresses, cards, `reauth`; fixtures `CHAT` `askAmara` `driveTo({ ask })` `completeJob` `earningsTo` `PAYOUT_DAYS`). Two readings the spec left open: sending a message marks the whole thread read for the sender (you have seen what you are answering, so Amara replying clears her own unread), and `removeAddress` returns the promoted address as an id (`newDefault`). Existing payout rows gained `createdAt`, so three M4 model tests compare payouts without it. Why: nothing downstream could link without it. Overrule: change the model, re-run `npm run check`.
- **D-146** (M5, integrator) DEFAULTED — The offline banner is part of every render: `app.js` inserts `systemBanner(state)` after the view and calls `wireSystem` after the screen's `wire`, skipped when a wire() already forwarded to another screen. Journeys send from the keyboard (Enter) because at the harness's 390 px width the Test-flows trigger, shown on the first keystroke, covers the Send button. Why: the system layer must reach all screens without touching any host. Overrule: per-screen banners (D-138).
- **D-147** (M5, fixer R1) DEFAULTED — The note under Sarah's / Amara's own masked bubble says what happened, not the rule: "Hidden from Amara. Numbers and emails stay private on Taily." (peer's first name; `chatBubble({ peer })`, passed by `threadMessages`; no peer → the standing `PRIVACY_NOTE`). The standing note at the top of the thread keeps the rule word for word, so the two lines no longer repeat each other. The note balances to two even lines (`text-wrap: balance`, 260 px). Chat photo buttons carry a ready-made `data-title` ("Your photos" / "Photos from Amara", singular for one) that `wirePhotoViewer` prefers over the garment form "<type> · photos". Any sheet or modal opening clears the toasts first, so a toast never covers a sheet control. Why: R5-1-C-01 / C-04 / C-08 / T-05 / T-08. Overrule: restore `PRIVACY_NOTE` in the bubble.
- **D-148** (M5, fixer R1) DEFAULTED — A payout the bank sent back is re-sent only when Amara saves bank details (`setPayouts` re-queues it; `settlePayouts` never retries a failed payout). (a kept deposit keeps "No Taily fee on a kept deposit."). Why: R5-1-T-07 / T-02. Overrule: restore the time in the header.

(`POLICY.CODE_TTL_MIN`, via a new `codeInput({ helper })` override) instead of repeating the lead. A resend countdown there was not used: the Resend link right under it already counts down. Screens whose rendered UI changed in M5 were rebuilt in place (ids kept): c-thread--new (824:4638, now with the "No booking yet" context row and question chips), t-thread (836:4813, M5 thread head with the full address, context row, system lines) and to-payouts (830:4561, "jobs you completed before that day", D-152). - **D-154** (M6, fixer, M6-X-01 + T-14 + P-25) DEFAULTED — A suggested time lapses. It stays open until the earlier of `MIN_LEAD_H` (3 h) before it starts and `POLICY.SUGGEST_EXPIRY_H` (24 h) after it was suggested (`suggestLapseAt(a)`; `suggested.at` is stored by `tailorSuggest`). Past that, `acceptSuggestion` fails `slot-passed` and `tick()` ends the request as a final expiry: status `expired`, `retries = MAX_RETRIES`, deposit hold `released`, the time back in `openSlots`, and the thread line "Amara’s suggested time passed" / "Your suggested time passed". The tailor can also withdraw it (`T.withdrawSuggestion`, only from `suggested`) with the same outcome and the line "Amara withdrew her suggested time" / "You withdrew your suggested time". Either ending hides the tailor for this job like any final expiry (D-07) and puts the job back in the draft when the draft is still empty (D-158). Why: D-14 let a suggestion wait forever while the deposit hold and the tailor’s slot stayed locked. Overrule: change `SUGGEST_EXPIRY_H`, or drop the hide (D-07) for a withdrawn time.
- **D-155** (M6, fixer, M6-T-01) DEFAULTED — Amends D-25: when the visit moves (the customer accepts a suggested time, or reschedules), rush = min(rush as booked, rush for the new slot), re-priced through `repriceMoney`. The price never goes up and never charges rush for work that is no longer rush; the captured deposit is unchanged (it excludes rush), so the balance and the tailor’s payout move by the rush alone. `tailorSuggest` stores `suggested.rush` with the same rule so the customer sees the price before she accepts. Why: D-25 froze the total even when a later slot made the work non-rush. Overrule: keep the booked price on every move (D-25).
- **D-156** (M6, fixer, M6-M-01 + M-03 + X-06) DEFAULTED — Amends D-23 / D-29: a refund below the deposit (the final order is worth less than what was held) is recorded as `payments.refund = { cents, state: 'pending' }` when the order is marked ready, re-priced by `chooseReturn` (a courier eats into it; the entry is removed at $0), and settled to `refunded` by `payBalance`. The customer’s refund row reads "After you choose" while pending and "Refunded" once paid (`customerBreakdown`, `refundState`). Invariant (model-tested): deposit + balance − refund = total. New scenario `SCENARIOS.priceBelowDeposit`. The screen copy for a $0 balance ("Nothing to pay", "Confirm and get $X back") is Batch 3’s. Why: the refund used to read as already paid at Ready, before the customer had chosen how to get the order back. Overrule: refund at Ready (D-23).
- **D-157** (M6, fixer, M6-M-04 + X-05 + M-10) DEFAULTED — Late cancel stays deposit-only: within 24 hours the deposit is kept and paid to the tailor in full, with no second charge and no separate home-visit fee kept. MASTER-PLAN §2 is locked and untouched; its parenthetical "(covers the non-refundable home-visit fee)" is descriptive only and is FLAGGED FOR KEVIN. The 24-hour edge is inclusive: exactly 24 h ahead is still "early" (`cancelTerms`, `rescheduleBooking`). Why: the code, the copy and §2’s rule all say deposit-only; the docs said otherwise. Tapping it focuses that appointment and opens its status screen. The wizard follows under a small "Book something else" label; an empty grid no longer repeats "Tap a garment to add it." as a helper under the grid (the lead already says it). Why: the app opened on a blank wizard while a booking waited on her one tab over. Overrule: leave Home as the wizard alone.
- **D-169** (M6, fixer, M6-C-18 + T-03) DEFAULTED — From the fitting onward the past visit time is demoted. On the customer side c-booking's in-progress, ready, returning and completed states drop the visit block (its date title, address and "n items · n services" count) for one small line under the hero, "Fitted Thu, Oct 15 · At my place"; the order and the receipt below it are what matter now. Fitting and final-order states keep the visit block (she is at the visit). On the tailor side (M6, Batch 4) t-job’s fitting and in-progress states lead with the due date instead of the visit: "Due Fri, Oct 23 · in 8 days" (warning ink within a day) over one small "Fitting was Thu, Oct 15"; t-home’s In progress rows read "<step> · due Fri, Oct 23" ("Started", "Order sent", "Tailoring", "Ready", or the return step) and sort soonest due first. Why: a time that is already over was the loudest thing on a screen about work in progress. Overrule: keep the visit block in every state.
- **D-170** (M6, fixer, M6-P-28) DEFAULTED — Amends D-106: the customer word for a home drop-off after the balance is paid is "Drop-off" in lists (short) and "Coming to you" on the detail screen and its timeline, not "Return scheduled" (there is no schedule in the model; the time is agreed in chat). The tailor's word stays "To drop off". The c-booking "Back with you" step caption follows `statusWord`, so it reads the same. Why: "scheduled" promised a time the model does not have. Overrule: restore "Return scheduled".
- **D-171** (M6, fixer, M6-P-37) DEFAULTED — Amends D-129 / D-149: a thread header's sub line carries the countdown only, never the word that the context row's pill already says — customer "2h left for Amara to reply", tailor "2h left to reply" (past the deadline the countdown component reads "Expired"). "Request sent" / "New request" live in the context pill. The tailor's question-before-booking context row reads "No request yet" with "Her request will show here when she books." Why: the header and the pill said the same word twice. Overrule: restore "Request sent ·" / "New request ·" in the header.
- **D-172** (M6, fixer, M6-T-06 + M-02 + M-09) DEFAULTED — Tailor money words. The per-job figure is **You take home** everywhere (the request card, the job’s breakdown total row, the completed job’s hero, to-services rows "You take home $22.50 · Taily fee $2.50", the t-payout breakdown). **Payout** is only the bank transfer (Earnings, t-payout’s hero, to-payouts). One state vocabulary for a payout on every surface, from `payoutLine(p, bank)` in tailor-data.js: Pending ("Arrives Fri, Oct 16 · to Chase ••6789"; "Sending again …" after a retry; "Sends … once your bank details are updated" while another payout is sent back), Paid ("Paid Fri, Oct 16 · to Chase ••6789") and Didn’t go through (error tone, "sends again once your bank details are updated"). The pill says Pending, never "Scheduled". A list row’s caption uses its `short` form without the bank. t-job’s completed card links a failed one to t-payout. Why: "payout" meant three things (a job’s share, the Friday transfer, the state of it) and a paid job still said "Pending". Overrule: restore "Your payout" on the job screens.
- **D-173** (M6, fixer, M6-T-15) DEFAULTED — The tailor’s visit block (accepted, confirmed, visit time) carries two small actions: **Directions** (home visits only: a maps link from `jobAddress(appt)`, `target="_blank" rel="noopener"`, the full street exists only after the accept, D-88) and **Add to calendar** (an .ics `data:` link with start, end and address). They are links, so `ctaSmall(label, { href })` renders an `<a class="cta-small">`. Why: the screen she opens on the way to a visit had the address as plain text and no way to put the visit in her calendar. Overrule: drop the row.
- **D-174** (M6, fixer, M6-P-34) DEFAULTED — Confirm sheets by weight. A non-destructive confirm (mark the order ready) is ✕ in the header plus ONE primary button, no "Not yet"; the intro uses `text-wrap: pretty`. A destructive sheet keeps D-79: one danger button and a "Keep …" outline button (cancel the visit, decline, withdraw a suggestion, mark a no-show). Why: "Not yet" was a second answer to a question nobody had asked twice, and its twin ✕ already closes. Overrule: restore the outline button.

- **K-02** (Kevin, Oct 2026) LOCKED — At the fitting, when the tailor reviews the job ("Edit the job"), each garment card lets her add **Before** and **Pinned** photos and a **comment**, following the v4 design (Before | Pinned tabs on the garment card; v4 refs `ref/t04-appointment-details.png`, `ref/t05-confirm-final-pricing.png`, `ref/04-review-approve*.png`). Why: tailors need to document their work. Every garment card on both sides shows its **garment-type icon** next to the title (e.g. the Suit Jacket card shows the suit-jacket icon from `js/garment-icons.js`). Director calls (D-K02a): the photos and comment travel with the final order to the customer (shown on "Review your final order" and the booking afterwards) and stay on the tailor's job; **Before and Pinned photos are REQUIRED (Kevin, follow-up)**: every garment in the final order (booked or newly added; not removed ones) needs at least 1 Before photo and at least 1 Pinned photo before "Send to Sarah for approval" is enabled. Missing photos are flagged on that garment's card, and the bar says how many items still need photos. The comment stays optional.
- **D-175** (K-02, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Where fitting documentation lives. The tailor's photos and comments are saved on the appointment the moment she makes them (`a.docs`, keyed by garment id, booked `g…` or draft new item `n…`), not in the t-adjust draft, so Back, leaving and coming back never lose them; the leave modal keeps D-123 for item / price edits only ("Your photos and comments are saved. Item and price changes aren’t."). `sendPriceChange` snapshots the docs of exactly the sent garments into `priceChange.docs`; the customer only ever sees that snapshot, so edits made while she is reviewing reach her only with "Send the updated order" (revise). Docs are editable at `fitting` and while the order is pending / messaged; after approval the snapshot is the locked record. Why: photos are real-world evidence that must not vanish with a draft, and the order Sarah approves must be exactly what she saw. Overrule: make docs live-shared (Sarah sees every photo as it is taken) and drop the snapshot.
- **D-176** (K-02, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Up to 4 Before and 4 Pinned photos per garment (`FITTING_LIMITS.PHOTOS_MAX`), 56 px tiles: four thumbnails plus the add tile fit one row of the 318 px card body, and v4 showed four. Comment up to 200 characters (`COMMENT_MAX`, same as the price note), trimmed, stored masked with `maskContact` like the note and chat. Why: one row keeps the card scannable at the fitting; 200 matches the other tailor free-text field. Overrule: change `FITTING_LIMITS` in `js/data.js` (the tiles wrap to a second row above 4).
- **D-177** (K-02, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Simulated capture: tapping a dashed camera tile adds a photo immediately (no picker sheet, no upload spinner). The placeholder is the garment's own illustration on one of four token tones, tilted slightly, with a pin badge on Pinned photos; the tone and tilt come from the appointment's photo sequence (`a.docSeq`), so screenshots are deterministic. Why: the prototype has no camera, and at a fitting one tap per photo is the real speed; the illustration makes "a photo of this garment" legible without image files. Overrule: open the M5 attach sheet (Take photo / Choose from library) before adding.
- **D-178** (K-02, spec writer) DEFAULTED — How "photos required" reads (Kevin's rule: ≥ 1 Before and ≥ 1 Pinned on every garment in the final order, booked or new, not removed ones). The model fails `photos-missing` (with the gaps) after `max-items` and before `note-long`. On t-adjust each live card shows "Add at least one before photo." / "…pinned photo." in error ink with the alert glyph next to the empty row, always visible (not `role="alert"`, it shows on load), and the Send button is disabled with the bar note "Add before and pinned photos for n items." as its `aria-describedby`. The money lead stays in the bar. The comment is optional and says so. Why: the requirement is said once per place: the card says what, the bar says how many. Overrule: keep Send enabled and, on press, scroll to the first card that is missing a photo.
- **D-179** (K-02, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Where the photos show. Tailor: t-adjust (editable), t-job "The work" from the fitting on (working docs at the fitting, the sent / approved snapshot after; read-only viewer), waiting / messaged in "The order you sent", and the completed job gains "The work" as her record. Customer: "Review your final order" and c-booking "Your order" (in progress, returning, completed). Not on c-ready (the pay step stays focused), c-pay (none exist yet), c-complete or thread lines (the system line "Amara sent your final order" does not mention photos). Why: both sides read the same ticket where the order is shown; the pay and wrap-up steps stay short. Overrule: add the docs block to c-ready's "Your order" too (one `docs` argument).
- **D-180** (K-02, spec writer) DEFAULTED — v4's Before | Pinned label pair becomes real tabs (`role="tablist"`, 44 px, counts in the labels, Before first and default; the selection is not remembered across re-renders). Tapping a photo opens a viewer sheet rather than v4's dark full-screen overlay: header "Suit Jacket · Before", who and when, the same tabs, a 3:4 stage, ‹ n of N ›, a thumbnail strip, and, for the tailor while editable, "Remove photo" (removes, closes, Undo toast). Why: v5's overlays are sheets (D-79 family), and tabs give the label pair a keyboard and screen-reader model. Overrule: restore v4's full-screen dark viewer.
- **D-181** (K-02, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Garment icons through one helper, `garmentIcon(type, { size, alt })`: 40 px on every garment head (t-adjust cards, changeList, workList; identical to c-job's existing tile), 32 px in sheet rows (Add an item), 24 px inline. The icon is decorative (`alt=""`, `aria-hidden`) wherever the garment name is printed next to it, so screen readers don't read the name twice (WCAG redundant alt), and `alt` = the garment name when it stands alone. Unknown types get a hanger glyph in the same well. Rows that are not garment cards keep no icon: c-tailor's estimate price list ("Suit Pant · Hem pants"), count lines ("2 items · 3 services"), threads, c-orders. Why: Kevin asked for the icon next to every garment title; a list of prices is not a title. Overrule: add `garmentIcon(type, { size: 'inline' })` to c-tailor's estimate rows.
- **D-182** (K-02, spec writer) DEFAULTED — A new item's id is never reused within an appointment: `nextItemId(appt)` looks past saved docs, sent lines and booked garments, and t-adjust seeds its draft counter from it. Docs of a discarded draft item stay in `a.docs` unseen (only garments in the sent lines are ever snapshotted). Why: otherwise a re-added item could inherit another item's photos. Overrule: delete docs when a draft new item is dropped (and lose Undo's photos).
- **D-183** (K-02, spec writer) DEFAULTED — When the tailor changes photos or comments after sending (while Sarah has not approved), t-job's waiting screen says "You changed photos or comments since you sent the order. Send the updated order so Sarah sees them.", and in t-adjust revise a docs change alone enables "Send the updated order". Why: with the snapshot (D-175), an unsent photo would otherwise silently never reach her. Overrule: publish doc edits to the sent order immediately (see D-175's overrule).
- **D-184** (K-02, builder) DEFAULTED — Three small build calls on K-02. (1) The dashed camera tile uses `--neutral-500` (the D-163 control-boundary ink), not v4's `--neutral-400`, so the tile meets 3:1 against white. (2) The comment trigger reads "+ Add comment" as text only (no ⊕ glyph beside it), matching "+ Add a service" and avoiding a doubled plus. (3) The viewer's photo is a centred 240 px wide, 3:4 stage rather than full width, so the tabs, controls, thumbnails and Remove photo fit one sheet without scrolling. Why: contrast, one plus per button, a sheet that fits. Overrule: set the tile border to `--neutral-400`, add `ICON_ADD_CIRCLE` before the label, remove the width cap on `.docs-viewer__photo`.
- **D-185** (K-02, fixer, K2-1-01 + 02 + 03) DEFAULTED — Fitting docs polish; photos stay required (D-178), nothing here makes them optional. (1) changeList / workList: every garment after the first is its own block, a `--neutral-200` hairline and 20 px above it (a new-item green panel keeps its own padding, no hairline); the read-only comment is a white panel with a 1 px `--neutral-200` ring so it shows on the `--neutral-50` page. Every list that prints more than one garment (c-pay, c-ready, c-booking, c-price-change, t-request, t-job) grew by the 20 px and was re-baselined. (2) The photo viewer stage is `clamp(160px, 70vh − 330px, 320px)` tall (3:4), so tabs, stage, controls, strip and Remove photo fit the 70vh sheet at 390×667 and 390×844 with no scroll. (3) `ctaSmall(label, { filled: true })` adds the ink-filled small CTA (`.cta-small--filled`); the comment editor's Save uses it, sized to its label (min 88 px), and Cancel is a 12 px w-600 quiet text button, both 44 px tall.

  - **Signature elements (only these two):** the **garment illustrations** (used wherever a garment appears — cards, booking, inbox, threads, empty states) and a **stitched dashed divider** (replaces hairlines on receipts / order / money breakdowns, used sparingly).

- **D-186** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Booking flow chrome: the top nav is hidden on booking steps 2–5 (c-where, c-tailors, c-tailor opened from the list, c-book, c-pay) and replaced by a 56 px flow bar (Back · "n of 5" · ✕ "Leave booking"). c-job keeps the nav because it is the Home tab. ✕ returns to c-job, keeps the draft job and asks nothing, because nothing has been sent. - **D-188** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Status = an 8 px dot plus a 12/600 word, in six warm tones: action (accent, with a halo and bold, the D-95 "your move"), pending (ochre), progress (sage), done (green), stop (brick), calm (grey). - **D-189** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — One "Verified" mark (seal glyph and word, success ink). On c-tailor, tapping it opens a "Verified by Taily" sheet listing ID verified, Background check and Insured, each with one line. On tailorRow the mark is inert because the row is already a button. The three green chips are removed. Why: K-03 #7. Overrule: change the sheet copy in A5.7.
- **D-190** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The rating prints once, as "★ 4.9 (37)" on the c-tailor head, linking to the Reviews section. Overrule: put the summary back in Reviews and drop it from the head.
- **D-191** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Reassurance copy moves into six ⓘ sheets (pricing, deposit, fitting, fee, payouts, privacy) opened by a 44 px ⓘ button. Copy that changes a decision stays inline: the deposit hold, the cancellation cut-off, every amount, "Only your neighborhood is shared…", "Full address after you accept" and "…hidden". Why: K-03 #4 asks for a cut of about 50%. Overrule: re-inline any line by deleting it from `INFO`.
- **D-192** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Section headings stay sentence case in source and are uppercased by CSS. Because the click-through matches `innerText`, every expect that asserts a section heading is rewritten in CAPS (listed in M7-spec §D1). Why: this keeps the copy source readable and the harness honest. Overrule: assert the CSS class instead of the text.
- **D-193** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — One depth strategy: interactive containers are white with a 1 px `--neutral-200` inset ring (`.card`), and reference content is flat on the linen. Drop shadows are removed from summary and trust cards. Shadows stay only on overlays (sheets, modals, toasts, push banner). Money breakdowns, timelines and info cards render flat. Why: K-03 #2, and "pick one depth strategy". Overrule: give `.card` a soft shadow instead of the ring.
- **D-194** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Every photo placeholder (job item photo, chat photos, review photos, portfolio, workList thumb, photo viewer, trust card) is a `photoStub`: the garment illustration on a soft token tint with a slight tilt, the same rendering as the K-02 `docPhoto`. Portfolio stubs cycle through `GARMENT_TYPES`. `--media-placeholder` is retired. Why: K-03 says placeholders must look intentional, and the garment illustrations are the signature. Overrule: use a hanger glyph everywhere.
- **D-195** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — One filled button per screen. Secondary actions become text links (accent ink), and destructive actions become red text links. Outline buttons survive only as the second button of a confirm sheet or modal, as Apple / Google on c-welcome, and in two-path empty states. c-complete loses its "Done" button because Back and the nav already leave. On t-job, "I can't make it" and "Mark as no-show" move from the "Something wrong?" block into a ••• menu in the back row. Why: K-03 #8, and both t-job actions are rare and destructive, which is the standard menu case. Overrule: restore the block on t-job.
- **D-196** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Fitting photo rows show a neutral "Required" until Amara taps Send or tries to leave. After that the row shows the error line and the camera tile gets a red ring. While photos are missing, Send is soft-disabled: it looks disabled and has `aria-disabled`, but a tap marks the draft as tried and focuses the first missing row. Send is still hard-disabled when there is nothing to resend. Why: this is the K-03 follow-up on K-02, because errors should not show before the person acts. Overrule: show the error after the first photo is added instead.
- **D-197** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — The stitched dash appears in two places only: directly above the total row of every `moneyRows` breakdown (customer, tailor, receipt, payout), and once under the return line on a receipt. List separators stay hairlines. Why: K-03 says to use it sparingly on receipts, orders and money. Overrule: also use it between receipt sections.
- **D-198** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — c-welcome drops the three-checkmark list for a Suit Jacket illustration on an accent circle and one line: "Real prices up front. Your deposit is held, not charged, until the tailor accepts." to-done swaps its ✓ circle for the same illustration. Why: K-03 #4 asks for a garment illustration and one line, and the line keeps the money fact. Overrule: pick another garment or line.
- **D-199** (M7, spec writer) DEFAULTED — Both personas' avatars use the same warm tint (accent-bg circle, accent-ink initials, faint accent ring). Past or closed rows keep the neutral tint. Why: K-03 says initials restyled warm and in a circle, and two tints would read as a status. Overrule: give customers a neutral tint.
- **D-200** (M7, integrator) DEFAULTED — Small M7 calls found at integration. (1) t-job, once the fitting has started, titles the visit-time block "Timing" (a `sectionHead` over "Fitting was Tue, Oct 13"), because the visit card's "Visit" heading would read wrong after the visit happened. (2) The ••• menu on t-job lists "Mark as no-show" before the visit's wait window is over; its sub-line says when it unlocks ("If Sarah isn’t there by 10:15") and tapping it before then shows a toast instead of opening the confirm sheet, so the row is never hidden and never misleading. (3) Shared components: `textLink()` takes `href` and renders an anchor with the same classes (t-job Directions / Add to calendar); `docsEditor`'s comment helper is only "Phone numbers and emails are hidden."; the Required label and the error line carry `tabindex="-1"` so t-adjust can focus the first missing-photo message after Send. (4) The harness: `js/inventory.js` gets `M7` in `MILESTONES` and `M6`, `M7` in `BUILT` (the index now lists their states), the generator no longer drops states after a "Before | Pinned" cell (three t-job states were missing), and `lint.mjs` fails on `--info` / `--media-placeholder` colour tokens and on a blurred `box-shadow` in v5 CSS outside overlays. Why: each keeps the spec's rule true without a new product call. Overrule: change the t-job copy or restore the always-hidden no-show row.

(1) `threadContext({ garment })` leads the card with a 32 px garment well (the linked booking's first garment); (2) `threadRow({ garment })` prefixes the preview line with a 24 px garment glyph in a `--neutral-50` well (drawn at 20). The avatar stays, because the person is the subject. A thread with no booking shows no garment. Every other row that prints "2 items · 3 services" stays text (the strip with "+n" was not built); `requestCard` and the t-earnings payout rows show the first garment at 32 px (`row`), the same well as t-payout. The completed receipt now lists the order (garment icon, services, final prices) between the return line and the totals, so its first stitch closes the header and the second closes the breakdown; `moneyRows` stitches only when a row precedes the total. Also: review photos with no named garment cycle the catalogue (never the hanger); the tailor's Fitting pill is `action` (photographing and sending is her move, D-95); the `calm` status dot is hollow so it differs from `progress` by shape; the unread count is folded into the nav link's aria-label; an unrated star is an outline; `[tabindex="-1"]` focus targets draw no UA ring. Why: M7-1 review findings C-01…C-08, T-01…T-08, A-01…A-07. Overrule: remove `garment` from the four call sites to go back to avatar-only rows.

(1) Before the fitting, c-request and c-booking's Visit block show the booked order as a `garmentStrip` (each garment in a 32 px white well, 8 apart, then "2 items · 3 services"), so the garment never disappears between c-pay and the approved order. (2) `apptRow({ garment })` puts a 24 px garment glyph before the time on c-bookings and c-orders. (3) `heroGarment(job)` picks the garment that stands for a booking in small wells (jacket, dress, shirt before skirt and pants), used by the inbox row (now a 32 px well before the preview), the thread card, the t-home request card, t-earnings and t-payout. (4) Change tags: "New", "New price" and "New item" share one accent tag (accent-bg ground, accent-ink, 10/600, sentence case so the visible words stay the same); the added row has no green tint and a new item's panel is a light accent wash — an added charge is not a success. (6) c-book's time wheel is the 3-row compact wheel with the settled row on top (`scheduler({ top })`), with no blank lead row. (7) t-adjust with nothing changed shows only the summary line and the breakdown, with no two-up figures; t-shop paused drops the preview caption, because the pill already says it. Why: M7-2 review findings C-01…C-07, T-01…T-04, A-01…A-05. Overrule: drop `garmentStrip` from the two visitRows to go back to text.

The nav icons keep the right end and their active state. Behaviour is the old one: `wireBackButtons` / `back()` / the screens' own `.back[data-act="back"]` hooks find the button in the nav unchanged. `back: false` keeps the logo (tab roots) or relies on the flow bar / onboarding bar (booking flow, onboarding: untouched). `topNav(active, items, { badges, back })` takes `back` for explicit use (gallery demo added). (2) The glyph is an SVG chevron on the nav icons' own 24 viewBox / 1.5 round stroke (`NAV_BACK`, icons.js), not the "‹" text glyph, so its weight equals the icons'; its stroke edge lands on the 20 px gutter. (3) Sign-in screens (c-phone, c-code, c-name, t-phone, t-code) have a status bar but no nav: `withNavBack` seats a Back-only bar (`topNav(null, [], { back: true })`) under the status bar, and their private `padding-top: calc(44px + 32px)` is dropped for the shared `--body-top`. (4) Heading: status line, title and notes are all flush left on the 20 px edge (the chevron row, its negative margins and the `heading-row--solo` class are gone); the status dot's halo is removed everywhere (`.status--action`; the dot now sits on the gutter edge level with the title) and the token `--status-action-halo` is left unused; status to title is `--rhythm-tight` (8 px; was 4 px, plus the old row's own 5 px negative pad, so the visible gap is about the same as K-04's but now exact). (5) `--body-top` stays 116: the old Back row had already been folded into the title row in K-04, so no dead space is left. (6) Thread heads (c-thread, t-thread) keep their sticky avatar + name row, now flush left; their Back is in the nav. Why: K-06, web app first, Back must be visible in-app. Overrule: restore the chevron inside `headingRow`; the marker plumbing can stay.

- **K-07** (Kevin, Oct 2026) LOCKED — The **status bar + top nav** and the **sticky bottom button bar** (action bar) use the beige page ground (`--neutral-50`) instead of white, for a seamless look. Separation: **hairline on scroll** — no line at rest; a 1px hairline (`--hairline`) fades in only when content is scrolled under the bar (top nav: when the page is scrolled from the top; action bar: when content continues below it). - **D-222** (K-07, fixer) DEFAULTED — **REVERSED by K-08** (the pinning in (3) and the nav hairline in (2) are removed; the beige ground in (1), the action-bar hairline-on-scroll `[data-more]` and `wireScrollEdges` minus `data-scrolled` stay; the flow bar and its status bar are now beige too). Chrome on the page ground, hairline on scroll. (1) `.status-bar`, `.top-nav` (incl. `.top-nav--back` and the sign-in Back-only bar) and `.action-bar` use `--neutral-50`; the permanent inset border / shadow is gone; the unread-dot halo is `--neutral-50` (the active stitch and the dot read fine on beige). (2) One generic mechanism: `wireScrollEdges(el)` in `js/app.js` (called from `render()`) sets `#screen[data-scrolled]` (scrollY > 0) and `#screen[data-more]` (content continues below the viewport) from one passive scroll listener, coalesced into one rAF (plus resize / ResizeObserver, skipped under `html.is-test` so a baseline is static); `.top-nav::after` (bottom) and `.action-bar::before` (top) are a 1px `--hairline` at opacity 0 that fades to 1 in 150 ms `--ease-out`; no transition under `prefers-reduced-motion` or `html.is-test`. (3) **CALL: the status bar, top nav and system banner are now PINNED** (`position: fixed`, centred in the 390 column) on screens with a top nav — before K-07 they were `absolute` and scrolled away with the page, so "content scrolled under the bar" could not happen. The sticky thread heading moves to `top: var(--chrome-h)` (plus the banner height when it shows). Under `html.is-test` they stay `absolute` (a full-page screenshot of a fixed bar lands wherever the page was scrolled). (4) Unchanged: the booking flow bar (`.flow-bar`, so the status bar above it stays white and both still scroll away), the onboarding bar, ••• menus, toasts, sheets. Baselines: all 286 `ref/v5` re-accepted (272 changed on disk).

- **D-223** (K-09 layout B1, fixer) DEFAULTED — Shared-CSS adaptation pass (LAYOUT-PLAN P3-4, P1-1, P1-2, P1-4, P2-4), zero pixel diff on all 286 `ref/v5` baselines. (1) Chrome geometry is tokens: `--status-h` 44, `--nav-h` 56, `--column` 390, `--chrome-h` and `--body-top` derived (100 / 116), `--z-chrome 2 / --z-sticky 5 / --z-banner 9 / --z-overlay 10 / --z-toast 30`; every `max-width: 390px` is `var(--column)`; `.sheet`, `.sheet-scrim`, `.modal`, `.modal-scrim` use 2 / 1 inside their overlay holder (the holder is the stacking context at `--z-overlay`). The sign-in, welcome, to-done and sys-index status-bar-only screens offset by `--status-h`, not a literal 44. (2) Overlays: `.sheet` is capped at `100% − --rhythm-major` and scrolls inside; the grabber row and header are sticky (a sticky `top` is measured from the scroller's content box, so the grabber row owns the sheet's 12 px top padding through padding + equal negative margin and sticks at −12, the header sticks under it); `.modal--leave` / `.modal--push` cap and scroll; `fitOverlay()` in `components.js` re-measures the holder on window and `visualViewport` resize and is torn down on every close path. (3) Zoom tier `@media (max-width: 300px)` (not container queries): nav (padding 12, no gap, `flex: 0 1 44px` / min 32; the logo also shrinks, 52 px, because tab roots carry the logo + 4 icons, not Back + 4), stars (`repeat(5, minmax(0, 44px))` because the rating card is only 130 px wide at 195, not the plan's 32 floor), day hours (wrap, no stranded dash, toggle label wraps beside its toggle), wheel columns min 32 with an ellipsising date column, t-adjust row (label on its own row), request card / garment head / tailor row wrap, status word wraps, rush tier and zip chip labels wrap (amounts stay whole). Nothing in the tier applies at 320 or wider. (4) Safe areas: `--safe-top` / `--safe-bottom`; action bar, thread composers, sheet and toast add `--safe-bottom`; under `display-mode: standalone` `--status-h` becomes `--safe-top`, so the nav, `--chrome-h`, `--body-top` and the system banner follow. `viewport-fit=cover` kept. Needs one manual installed-app check on a notched iPhone. (5) Line-heights: every `font-size: var(--size-N); line-height: Mpx` pair in `components*.css`, `base.css` and `css/screens/*.css` is `var(--lh-N)` (new unitless-ratio tokens; `--lh-16-roomy`, `--lh-12-roomy`, `--lh-24-open` for the 24/16, 20/12, 31/24 outliers); fixed-height icon boxes (`line-height` = `height`) keep px. Why: LAYOUT-PLAN §1.4 rules 1–3, 6, 7. Overrule: per item.

(3) Every bare `margin-top: var(--space-16|24)` in `css/screens/*` is a role: banners / alerts under a heading, lists and cards under a heading, scheduler and delta blocks = block (24); fine print and error banners under their own group, filter / bar / banner stacks on c-tailors, the c-job grid under its lead, wait lines under a code field, legal under a CTA = group (12). - **D-246** (infra, cache-busting) DEFAULTED — GitHub Pages caches every file for 10 minutes, so a browser could pair a new `components.js` with an old screen module or stylesheet. `scripts/v5/stamp.mjs` (`npm run stamp`) now content-hashes the app: every local stylesheet link, modulepreload and the main module script in `index.html` / `components.html` gets `?v=<first 10 hex of sha1(file)>`, and an import map between `<!-- stamp:importmap -->` markers (before any modulepreload / module script) maps every runtime `./js/**/*.js` (not `js/v4`, `js/screens/v4`) to its hashed URL, so static and dynamic imports (`import(`./screens/${id}.js`)`) all load the hashed file. Idempotent and deterministic; `--check` exits 1 when stale and is the first step of `npm run check`. `scripts/serve.mjs` and `scripts/v5/lib/server.mjs` already route on the URL pathname only, so the query is ignored there. Why: one hash set per `index.html` revision = one consistent set of files per load. Overrule: delete the importmap block and the `?v=` strings and drop the `stamp` step from `check.mjs`.

Calls: (a) **One popover**: `anchorPopover(btn, html, { id, cls, label, aim })` + `closeAnchorPopover()` in components.js replace the K-15 Verified-only code; shell `.anchor-pop` (+ `.anchor-pop__arrow`) with two skins, `.verified-pop` (unchanged look) and `.garment-pop`; one slot (`activePop`), so opening either closes the other; `closeOverlay()` closes it. `openVerifiedPopover`, `closeVerifiedPopover`, `wireVerified` stay as names (the last two are aliases); app.js now calls `wirePopovers(el)`, which delegates `[data-verified]` and `[data-peek]`. A peek trigger carries its items as JSON in `data-peek` (`peekAttrs(items, label)`), the dialog name in `data-peek-label`; ids are `verified-pop` / `garment-pop` (each trigger's aria-controls). (b) A second tap now also puts focus back on the trigger (K-15 left it where the click put it; same in Chrome, fixes Safari). The Verified fade now runs only under prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference and never under `html.is-test` (it ran unless reduce); c-booking--verified-open's diff equals c-booking's thumb-stroke diff (1697 px), the popover itself 0 px. (c) **Strip**: `garmentStrip(items)` takes `{ type, services }` from `returnedItems(a)` (the approved lines once they exist) on c-booking (every state that shows the Visit block: confirmed, rescheduled, fitting, cancelled, cancelled late, can't make it, no-show) and c-request (every state). (d) **Card**: min width 160, max 280 (column − 24 at 320); several items scroll past 320 px (8 × 40) and the list then becomes a focusable `role="region"` "…, scroll for more"; dialog names = the garment / "Sarah’s order" / "2 more items". The stitch dash sits at half a line − 1 px (crisp at 2×). (e) **t-home card**: `<div class="request-card card">` + `.request-card__link` (an empty button over the whole card, z 1, carrying `data-act` / `data-id` and the card's old aria-label); the strip, header and time are aria-hidden (the link says it, as the old card button did); the cluster is `<button class="request-card__count peek-cluster">` (z 2, ::before pad to 44 px, label "2 items, 3 services: show what’s in the order"), count underlined neutral-500, 2 px ink when open; the press scale moved to `.request-card:has(> .request-card__link:active)`. Applies to the New requests and Waiting on Sarah cards; the gallery card passes items too. (f) Hover (hover-capable only): thumb stroke ink, cluster underline ink; the thumb press scale is motion-safe and off under test. (g) **Tests**: flows `c-booking--item-peek` (Suit Jacket), `c-request--item-peek` (Suit Pant · 1 service · Hem pants), `t-home--item-peek` (both items), inventory kind `overlay`, SCREENS.md; the flow runner has no key step, so toggle / move / Escape-returns-focus / outside tap / flip above / 12 px clamp / one slot with Verified / the card still opens t-request are journey **J14**. Re-baselined (thumb stroke + 12 gap, count underline, index / gallery): c-request (+ --withdraw, --soon, --suggested, --declined, --expired, --expired-2, --expired-final, --expired-passed, --suggested-passed), c-booking (+ --confirmed, --verified-open, --cancelled, --cancelled-late, --cant-make-it, --no-show, --fitting), t-home (+ --push-denied, --push, --no-requests, --no-upcoming, --soon, --suggested, --suggested-soon, --paused, --retried), sys-offline-tailor, sys-index, sys-components. The input is `aria-describedby` the slot; on error the slot IS the error (`field__msg--error service-row__pay`, role alert, `price-<id>-msg`, box ringed in the error tone); clearing the error (typing) swaps the slot back in place. No "Taily fee −$X" on the row any more (`feeText` / `feeCents` removed; D-70's one precision now spans the payouts only); the fee stays said once above the list. **Range wrap**: the amount ("$20–$30.") is one nowrap span. One line is not reachable at 390: the line needs 181 px, the name column leaves 170 (it would need the money column + gap ≤ 101 px while the payout alone is 107), so on a ticked row it breaks cleanly before the amount ("Most NYC tailors charge / $20–$30."), at 320 "Most NYC tailors / charge $20–$30."; never inside the amount. Unticked rows keep it on one line. 195 tier: the box and slot run the full width under the name as before. **(B) c-ready + post-payment status by the way back** (Kevin: "I'd rather they match"): `CHARGE_CAPTION` moved from c-ready.js to `js/components.js` with `chargedWhen(wayBack)` ("Charged at pickup" / "Charged on delivery", "Charged at handoff" only while no way back is chosen) — ONE map for the c-ready Pay caption + pay sheet, the Price → Balance row (`customerBreakdown(…, { wayBack })`, ready stage follows the on-screen choice incl. a pre-chosen single offer; held stage follows `returnOption`), the c-booking head label + timeline line ("Payment $144.00 · Charged at pickup") and the c-bookings row caption. Kept: the deposit hold wording, the tailor side, the toasts / thread sentences "You’ll be charged $X at handoff." (prose, not a status), "Charged" after a 7-day capture. **(C) K-46 = B1, the two-line Pay button**: `cta(label, { sub, subId })` (+ `actionBar({ ctaSub })`) in `js/components.js`, `.cta--two / .cta__main / .cta__sub` in `components-v5.css` (min-height 54, grows only when a line wraps; padding 8 / 16; sub 12 / 500 neutral-300 on ink; disabled sub neutral-600 / 600 on neutral-200), shown in the sys-components gallery (CTA + Action bar). c-ready's action bar and pay sheet use it: "Pay $144.00" over `CHARGE_CAPTION[way back]`; while Pay is disabled its second line is the existing reason ("Choose how to get it back first.", "Pick a pickup time first.", "Pick a delivery window first. The courier fee is added then.", the courier wait / failure lines); the left caption above the button is gone, so the bar is 139 px in every state at 390 (155 at 320 when the long courier reason wraps). A11y: the button is named by its first line (`aria-labelledby=<subId>-main`) and described by the second (`aria-describedby=<subId>`, `action-bar-sub-pay` / `pay-sheet-sub`), so the reason is read on the disabled button too; busy keeps the second line (no height jump). A $0 balance (Confirm …) keeps its lead + one-line button; "Use another card" (the selected card just failed) is one line with no lead (it is not a charge). Why: K-45; K-46; Kevin's follow-ups. Overrule: a wider money column (one-line range only if the payout leaves the row, i.e. P2); the slot's two-line form; a different fallback label before a way back is chosen; a live sr-only note in addition to the description.

Legal items marked counsel are flagged in docs/v5/COUNSEL.md for Kevin's next review (not decided in code). Decisions:
  - **D-01 → K-48.1**: Taily support = an in-app **Help chat** (order attached) that **notifies the CEO (Kevin, admin)** who resolves it "almost immediately". Prototype: a Help thread per case + an admin view to act on it (refund / keep deposit, release / capture hold, cancel, pause payout, reply).

  - **D-11 → K-48.5**: approved ("Did the visit happen?" at start + 2 h to both), and **if unanswered after 4 hours the admin is notified** (in addition to the recommended 24 h cancel-with-refund).
  - **D-16 → K-48.6**: to-services intro copy: **"You can change it at the fitting, and the customer approves the final order before you start."**
  - **D-17 → K-48.7**: approved (tailor screens say "charged at pickup" / "on delivery", Sarah's words). The **deposit CTA** reads **"Reserve Appointment - $16.00 Deposit"** — mockups first (scratchpad k51), build after Kevin picks.

- **D-310** (pre-MVP A1, K-48: D-02 + DG-2 A + K-48.4 + Kevin's text timings) DEFAULTED — **Every notification is a text, fired through one function.** `js/texts.js` is the catalogue (87 rows: event × recipient × the words without "Taily: " × trigger × landing screen × owner; `textCtx` builds what a body may print) and `docs/v5/TEXTS.md` its generated table (`node scripts/v5/texts-doc.mjs`); `state.js notify(appt, event, { at, x })` renders a row into `state.outbox` (`{ id, seq, event, to, apptId, key, body, link, at, land, status }`, the K-43 `textLink(apptId, event)` link, `event:n` for a repeat) — status `delivered` when the person's texts are on, `off` when not (the in-app fallback: the screens already show the state), `undelivered` with `sim.sms = 'undelivered'`. Every transition / timer that is news for the other person (or a reminder) calls it (A2 / B1 fire their rows — `taily-replied`, `case-decided`, `verified`, `insurance-expiring` — the same way). **K-48.4: no quiet hours** (none existed in code; the catalogue's "When" column says "at any hour" by construction). Display: `system.js` draws the newest delivered text for the person on screen after every Demo action (`showNewText`, replaces flow-menu's `remindText`) and live while `sim.texts` is 'on' (`liveTexts`, replaces `textNewRequests`; same new-request words); tapping lands on the row's screen (else statusScreen). **Opt-in (D-02):** `state.texts = { customer, tailor }` (`on`, `stoppedAt`, `changedAt`); booking texts are the customer's own opt-in — an unticked "Text me booking updates" box on c-name (new accounts; `A.saveName({ texts })`), Account → Texts (`c-texts`, new) — and the tailor's on t-texts (new; Shop → Account → Texts); `setTexts(who, on)` (on = a confirmation text), `textReply(who, 'STOP'|'START'|'HELP')` (sim of a reply; Demo action "Reply STOP"); c-phone's helper now covers sign-in codes only. "Texts are off" banners on t-home (requests still expire in 2 h) and c-bookings (never-opted-in vs STOP wording), each → the Texts screen. **The K-43 auto-cancel needs a delivered reminder** (`reminder.texted`, `autoCancelArmed(a)`): otherwise the plain 24 h rule, the nudge says "It's still on", and c-job / c-booking / t-job print the unarmed copy ("You haven't confirmed · The visit is still on…"); c-pay's SF-04 line says the day-before ask is in the app when texts are off. **Kevin's timings:** the tailor's `start-soon` text START_REMIND_MIN (60) before the visit; at the start time ONE text, `fitting-started` "Your fitting has started. Please take all notes here:" (sent by the clock, not by Start fitting; it stands in for a second start reminder — the merge rule; a clock that jumped past the start skips the first, past the visit check skips both); chat `message` / `message-t` "Sarah sent you a message. Reply here:" for the first unread message, then at most one per thread per CHAT_TEXT_GAP_MIN (15) while unread (no message body in the SMS — the words stay in the masked chat). Other new texts beyond k50's 43: request-soon, suggestion-ended, picked-up-marked, job-done-t, pickup-today (PICKUP_REMIND_H 2), the D-03 / D-04 / D-11 / D-12 / S-32 / S-35 rows. `sys-texts` (new) renders the whole catalogue with the real banner. Why: D-02 approved, K-48.4, K-43 (web app first), Kevin's follow-ups via the coordinator. Overrule: per row in js/texts.js (words, trigger, landing) and POLICY (START_REMIND_MIN, CHAT_TEXT_GAP_MIN, PICKUP_REMIND_H); include the message body in chat texts; pre-tick the opt-in (not recommended: carriers).

The card is the K-28 `reminderCard` with a new `dismissAct` variant (a 44 px ✕ top-right, `.notice-card`); ✕ or its button calls `dismissNotice(id, persona)` (`a.noticeSeen[persona]`, model state). Your 2 items and 3 services are saved…" Find another tailor; "We cancelled your 5:00 PM visit · You didn't confirm by 2:00 PM…" Book Amara again), t-home under the summary ("Sarah cancelled today's 5:00 PM visit · …you keep her $16.00 deposit. It comes with your payout on Fri, Oct 16." See the visit). P-04: the review reminder card gets the same ✕ (`dismissReviewReminder`), and an abandoned order never asks for a review. - **D-312** (pre-MVP A1, D-03 / B-07, K-30 unchanged) DEFAULTED — **Every waiting state ends, in tick().** Final order (price-change, pending or messaged): texts at WAIT_REMIND_1_H 24 / WAIT_REMIND_2_H 72 after the LATEST send (a revision restarts the clock), then at FINAL_ORDER_REVIEW_D 7 days a Taily review (both texted, the admin notified); approving closes it. Edit pending: texts at 24 / 72 h; the tailor can **Withdraw changes** (`withdrawJobEdit`, a confirm sheet on t-job; Sarah is texted; Mark ready opens). Ready with no way back: texts at 24 / 72 h; at READY_DEFAULT_D 14 days `defaultReturn` sets in-store pickup and HOLDS the balance on the booking card with no time yet (`handoff.slot` null, `defaultedAt`) — the capture clock starts; c-booking "We set it to in-store pickup · pick a time"; no shop → a Taily review instead; a declining card leaves it ready with the card-failed texts. Unclaimed: the day-6 capture **pays the tailor then** (one payout; the handoff never adds a second); ABANDONED_CLOSE_D 30 days after that charge the order closes (`completedBy: 'abandoned'`, statusScreen → c-booking "Order closed", t-job "Order closed · Not collected within 30 days of the charge"). A handoff problem open PROBLEM_REVIEW_H 72 → review. When the clock jumps past several reminders, only the latest is sent. **Reviews:** `a.review` is already the customer's star review, so a Taily case lives on `a.tailyReview = { reason, openedAt, by, note, closedAt, outcome? }`; `openReviews()` lists the open ones (`{ apptId, appt, reason, openedAt, by, note }`, oldest first), `tailyReviewOf(a)`; every case also pushes `state.admin.notifications` (`{ id, kind, apptId, text, at, read }`). Reasons: final-order-unanswered, problem-open, visit-unanswered, visit-disputed, no-show-disputed, tailor-no-show-disputed, delivery-missing, ready-no-way-back. The review UI is track A2's. B-07's "Overdue · due Fri, Oct 23" on t-home / c-bookings / Home rows (`overdue(a)`). Why: D-03 approved. A charge can fail (`sim.capture = 'declined'`, one-shot) → `balance.state 'capture-failed'`: at a handoff the order still completes (she has it) but statusScreen sends Sarah to c-booking ("Your card needs attention · …Update your card to finish." + Update card), no payout until paid; at the day-6 capture it stays open with the error banner ("Update it to keep your pickup"). - **D-332** (pre-MVP A2, D-01) DEFAULTED — **What the admin can do, in dollars, each a pure transition on the case's order** (`adminActions(c)` lists the open ones, `adminAct(caseId, kind)` runs one): **refund deposit** (a cancelled or completed order; drops unpaid no-show / kept-deposit payouts; a live order is refunded by cancelling it — `order-open`), **keep deposit** (a kept deposit stays; held payouts released), **release hold** (a held balance: she isn't charged; the order closes as `cancel.reason 'taily'`, nothing paid out, the deposit untouched — refund it separately), **capture hold** (she got it: completes like a handoff, `completedBy 'taily'`, the payout queues; a day-6 captured balance just completes), **cancel the order** (confirmed … awaiting-handoff: deposit refunded, a held balance released / a captured one refunded, payouts dropped, the items back as her draft), **pause / release payout** (`held`, the Friday run skips it), **reply** (to either side; texted `taily-replied` / `taily-replied-t`, `help-replied(-t)` with no order), **close with outcome** — the decision line per side in dollars (both sides once money moved on the order, whoever opened it), posted and texted (`case-decided` / `taily-replied-t`), the order's `tailyReview` closed with `outcome` = the last money action (or 'resolved' / 'no-change'); refused with `decide-first` while any payout is still held. c-booking / t-job say "Taily cancelled this order / job" with the amounts. - **D-430** (MVP readiness L, K-53 Q10) DEFAULTED — **Help hours and a backup admin.** Help's Contact Taily card: Text · Email · **Help hours** (Every day, 8:00 AM to 9:00 PM), then "A second Taily team member answers when we’re away, so a reply never waits on one person. Outside these hours we still read safety reports. In an emergency, call 911 first." (`HELP_CONTACT.backup` / `.after`, PROTOTYPE placeholders). A backup admin account is one more `ADMIN_ACCOUNT`-style row when it exists. Why: K-53 Q10. Overrule: name the backup person.
- **D-431** (MVP readiness L, bug) DEFAULTED — **The tailor's preview after a request.** After a request is sent the customer's draft is empty, so t-shop's "What customers see" priced nothing but the home fee and read "Example: , home visit". `previewJob(job)` (tailor-data.js): the draft while it has items, else the seed sample's items on the same ZIP and visit type; t-shop and to-done use it. Why: the known bug. Overrule: none.
- **D-432** (MVP readiness, L + M integrator) DEFAULTED — **Handoff times read her availability.** Track M's `handoffSlots` (pickup times and courier windows inside the ready hold) now skips a day off (`timeOffOn(t, day)`, D-426) and, as before, offers every window of a day (a split day: both windows, never the break, D-424). Minimum notice (D-425) stays a booking rule only: a pickup time is the customer collecting from an open shop, not a new visit. Model test "L + M merge". Why: K-53 a4 + d2 meet — a time inside the hold on her day off can't be kept. Overrule: apply her minimum notice to handoff times too.
- **D-433** (MVP readiness, L + M integrator) DEFAULTED — **The legal drafts state the hold at Ready.** L's full drafts (D-429) are kept; their money facts follow track M exactly: Cancellation and refunds → The balance (hold placed when the tailor marks it ready, not a charge; pickup or delivery chosen within `HOLD_DAYS` days, every time offered ends by the hold's last day; charged at pickup with the code / on delivery; not handed over by the last day → charged then, and with no way back chosen set to in-store pickup (no shop: Taily arranges the return); a hold that fails → update the card; the courier re-priced at Pay, a pricier window after Pay its own charge); the customer's Payments and holds page (Help) says the same in two lines (it no longer says the hold comes with the way-back choice); Terms §4 adds "or 4 days after it was ready"; the tailor's Payments page and the Tailor agreement §4: the payout goes the Friday after the handoff, or after the last-day charge. No-show = the deposit (D-407) and the free window that confirming doesn't end (D-409) were already in both. The agreement's fee reads "$15.00 of every $100.00" (derived, `tailyFeeFor`). Why: K-53 b + d2. Overrule: copy in js/policies.js.
- **D-434** (MVP readiness, L + M integrator) DEFAULTED — **One admin tools row on both admin tabs.** components.js `adminTools({ testFlows })` (Test flows while the tools are on · Sign out) closes a-inbox AND a-ledger; app.js `wireAdminTools(root)` wires it once (Test flows → the panel; Sign out → `adminSignOut`, `lockTools`, a-login). The nav stays Inbox · Ledger (M); a-ledger sits behind L's admin gate like every a-* screen. Why: K-52 / K-53 a8 + c7 — his session's controls shouldn't vanish on the second tab. Overrule: move Test flows / Sign out into the nav or a menu.
- **D-435** (MVP readiness, L + M integrator) DEFAULTED — **Journey numbers.** Both tracks wrote J34–J36; M's keep J34–J36 (hold at Ready, never chosen, courier at Pay) and L's become J37–J40 (log in, unknown number, the admin gate, time off + notice). The study boots through `?study=`, an admin test session in L's model (D-422), so its 39 states run with the tools on. Why: one numbering. Overrule: none.
- **D-440** (pre-MVP sweep, B-02) DEFAULTED — **Versioned approvals.** Every approval names what the customer saw: `approvePriceChange(id, { revision })` and `approveJobEdit(id, { revision })` (the `revisions` counter the tailor's re-send bumps, D-101 / default 13) and `answerProposal(id, accept, { start })` (a move the tailor can re-ask, D-316) fail `'revised'` (`{ revision, total }` / `{ start }`) when it is no longer current, so nothing is applied. c-price-change records the revision it painted (read when it is wired) and on `revised` re-renders the newer order with a warning banner under the header — "Amara updated the order while you were looking · New total $245.00 (was $205.00). Check it before you approve." (no figures when the total didn't move: "Check what changed before you approve.") — plus a warning toast; c-booking's Accept / Keep toasts "Amara just asked for <time> instead. Check it before you answer." Calls without a version (old callers, journeys) still apply the current order. PROTOTYPE: `sim.race` 'revised' (one-shot) = the tailor's phone re-sent the order (every line +$10.00) a moment before the approval arrived; flows `c-price-change--revised`, `c-price-change--edit-revised`. `acceptSuggestion` takes no version (a suggestion can't be revised, only withdrawn → `not-suggested`); the courier re-quote already shows the new price first (D-403). Production: the API carries the same version (optimistic concurrency). Why: B-02 (K-48 "fix everything"). Overrule: auto-apply the newest revision with a notice instead of bouncing.
- **D-441** (pre-MVP sweep, B-07's last piece) DEFAULTED — **"On the way" on Earnings.** `tailor-data.js inFlightView()` = her booked jobs not paid yet (confirmed through awaiting-handoff, no payout row), each with `a.money.payout` (what she takes home on the order as it stands, after the Taily fee); t-earnings shows them between the next payout and Paid (and under "No earnings yet"): "ON THE WAY · 1 job · $141.25 after the Taily fee, so far. Each is paid the Friday after it’s complete." with one row per job (customer, status word, items, amount) that opens t-job. Flow `t-earnings--on-the-way`. Why: B-07 proposed it so money Taily holds is never invisible to her; the rest of B-07 was D-312 / D-400…D-402. Overrule: drop the section, or show only jobs past the fitting (when the payout is final).
- **D-442** (pre-MVP sweep, S-18) DEFAULTED — **The tailor row is a stretched-link card.** `tailorRow` renders an `<article class="tailor-row card">` whose one action is `.tailor-row__link` (an empty `<button data-act="open-tailor">` laid over the card, labelled with what the card says: name, rating or years, estimate, place, next opening — the K-25 request-card pattern) and the face is `aria-hidden` except the Verified check, now always a real `<button>` (`verifiedCheck`'s `inline` span variant is removed) sitting above the link (`z-index` 2). The P-31 tap ring moves to `.card.tailor-row` (a declined row keeps it at 55 % like before); a declined row has no link and reads as text. No visual change. Why: S-18 (nested interactive content). Overrule: none expected.
- **D-443** (pre-MVP sweep, S-17 before-MVP half, a8 rem type) DEFAULTED — **Type follows the reader's text size.** `--size-*` tokens are rem on the browser's 16 px root (`--size-16: 1rem`; line heights were already unitless `--lh-*`); spacing, radii and the 44 px targets stay px. The layout tiers that reflow text are em media queries (300 → 18.75em, 340 → 21.25em, 359 → 22.4375em, 374 → 23.375em), so a doubled text size (390 px = 12.2em) gets the 200 % zoom reflow and the c-tailors filter pills wrap; the flow-menu (430 px) and desktop (600 px) queries stay px (viewport, not text). Garment tiles are `min-height: 110px` (were a fixed 110). Identical pixels at the default size, so no baseline moves. Why: S-17 + K-53 a8 (approved). Overrule: px tokens back (one block in tokens.css).
- **D-444** (pre-MVP sweep, K-53 a8) DEFAULTED — **The web-app shell.** `manifest.json` (name Taily, standalone, portrait, start_url / scope `./` for GitHub Pages, background + theme `#F7F4EF` = `--neutral-50`, icon `assets/app-icon.svg` — the wordmark on the page ground, maskable-safe), `<meta name="theme-color">`, the Apple web-app metas and the icon links in index.html. **The drawn 9:41 status bar is prototype chrome**: drawn only under `html.device-frame` — set by app.js with the admin's tools (every `?test=1` / `?flow=` / `?study=` boot, so every harness render and baseline keeps it) and statically on components.html; a real visitor's tab hides it and `--status-h` becomes the device's top inset (as installed, `display-mode: standalone`, already did). Why: K-53 a8 ("other a8 items approved"); keeping the bar in harness renders keeps all 600+ baselines. Overrule: draw it everywhere again (drop the two base.css rules), or hide it in test sessions too (re-baseline everything).
- **D-445** (pre-MVP sweep, K-53 a8) DEFAULTED — **The per-appointment deep link is left for the build, with its contract.** The prototype has no persisted appointments (a reload re-runs the flow), so a cold `/r/<code>` can't resolve. Contract for the build: every text's link is a signed, expiring token `taily.app/r/<token>` (today `textLink(apptId, event)` is a deterministic 6-character stand-in) that the server resolves to `(apptId, recipient)`; the app then renders `statusScreen(appt, persona)` — the same map the prototype's text banner already lands on (D-310) — after sign-in when the session is gone (sys-session-expired). Why: a8 lists it, but it needs a backend. Overrule: a prototype-only `?appt=<id>` boot param on top of a flow.
